<script>
/* Zulora Motion Engine. Turns ONE existing picture into a short motion preview, in the browser (WebGL), no new picture, no server, no cost.
   Styles: 0 = 2D (cinematic), 1 = 3D (estimated depth), 2 = simulation, 3 = pitch.  A "plan" (from the AI reader, or defaults) steers it.
   plan = { focus:{x,y,r}, accent:"#rrggbb", intensity:0.2-1.5, pitch:{title, points:[..], tagline} }   x,y,r are 0-1 (y from the top). */
(function(root){
const STYLES={'2d':0,'3d':1,'simulation':2,'pitch':3};
const LABEL={'2d':'2D · cinematic','3d':'3D · estimated depth','simulation':'Simulation · illustrative','pitch':'Pitch'};
const VS='attribute vec2 a;varying vec2 uv;void main(){uv=a*.5+.5;gl_Position=vec4(a,0.,1.);}';
const FS=`
#ifdef GL_FRAGMENT_PRECISION_HIGH
precision highp float;
#else
precision mediump float;
#endif
varying vec2 uv;uniform sampler2D uImg,uBlur,uDepth;uniform vec2 uR;uniform float uT,uP,uS,uI,uFade;uniform vec2 uF;uniform float uFR;uniform vec3 uAcc;
float hash(vec2 p){p=fract(p*vec2(123.34,456.21));p+=dot(p,p+45.32);return fract(p.x*p.y);}
float noise(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);return mix(mix(hash(i),hash(i+vec2(1,0)),f.x),mix(hash(i+vec2(0,1)),hash(i+vec2(1,1)),f.x),f.y);}
void main(){
 vec2 f=vec2(uF.x,1.-uF.y);float e=uP*uP*(3.-2.*uP);vec3 col;vec3 add=vec3(0.);
 if(uS<.5){ // 2D: slow push-in toward the focus, tiny drift, soft bloom
  float z=1.+.22*uI*e;vec2 p=f+(uv-f)/z+vec2(sin(uT*.5),cos(uT*.4))*.004;
  col=texture2D(uImg,p).rgb;col+=max(texture2D(uBlur,p).rgb-.62,0.)*.4;
  add+=uAcc*.10*smoothstep(1.,0.,length(uv-vec2(.2+.6*uP,.85)))*.6;
 }else if(uS<1.5){ // 3D: camera glides around the picture, depth shifts things by different amounts
  float a=6.2831*uP*.75;vec2 cam=vec2(sin(a),.55*cos(a))*.05*uI;
  float z=1.+.10*e;vec2 u2=f+(uv-f)/z;
  vec2 delta=cam/32.;float cur=0.;vec2 q=u2;float d=1.-texture2D(uDepth,q).r;
  for(int i=0;i<32;i++){if(cur>=d)break;q-=delta;d=1.-texture2D(uDepth,q).r;cur+=1./32.;}
  q=clamp(q,vec2(.002),vec2(.998));
  float h=texture2D(uDepth,q).r;float fh=texture2D(uDepth,f).r;
  vec3 sharp=texture2D(uImg,q).rgb,soft=texture2D(uBlur,q).rgb;
  col=mix(sharp,soft,smoothstep(.18,.65,abs(h-fh))*.7);col*=mix(.9,1.08,h);
  col+=max(soft-.55,0.)*.5*h;
 }else if(uS<2.5){ // simulation: flowing field, rings spreading from the focus, drifting light particles, faint grid
  vec2 w=vec2(noise(uv*3.+vec2(uT*.10,0.)),noise(uv*3.+vec2(5.2,uT*.10)))-.5;
  float s=(.006+.026*e)*uI;float z=1.+.08*e;vec2 p=f+(uv-f)/z+w*s*2.;
  col=texture2D(uImg,p).rgb;
  float r=length((uv-f)*vec2(uR.x/uR.y,1.));float ring=0.;
  for(int k=0;k<3;k++){float rad=fract(uP*1.6+float(k)/3.)*.95;ring+=exp(-pow((r-rad)*26.,2.))*(1.-rad/.95);}
  add+=uAcc*ring*.55;
  vec2 gg=abs(fract(uv*16.)-.5);float line=smoothstep(.488,.5,max(gg.x,gg.y));add+=uAcc*line*(.05+ring*.5);
  for(int L=0;L<3;L++){float sc=14.+float(L)*9.;vec2 g=(uv+w*.4)*sc+vec2(0.,-uT*(.25+.15*float(L)));vec2 id=floor(g);vec2 fr=fract(g)-.5;
   float hh=hash(id+float(L)*17.);vec2 off=(vec2(hash(id*1.3),hash(id*2.1))-.5)*.6;float dd=length(fr-off);
   float tw=.5+.5*sin(uT*2.+hh*6.28);add+=uAcc*smoothstep(.09,0.,dd)*step(.55,hh)*tw*(.55+.25*float(L));}
  col+=max(texture2D(uBlur,p).rgb-.55,0.)*.5;
 }else{ // pitch: calm push, darker lower third for the words
  float z=1.+.12*e;vec2 p=f+(uv-f)/z+vec2(-.02*e,0.);col=texture2D(uImg,p).rgb;
  col*=mix(1.,.3,smoothstep(.6,0.,uv.y));col+=max(texture2D(uBlur,p).rgb-.55,0.)*.4;
 }
 col+=add;
 vec2 v=uv-.5;col*=1.-dot(v,v)*.9;
 col+=(hash(uv*uR+uT)-.5)*.025;
 col=pow(max(col,0.),vec3(.97));
 gl_FragColor=vec4(col*uFade,1.);}`;

function hex(h){h=(h||'#ffcf7a').replace('#','');return [parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16)/255,parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16)/255,parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16)/255]}
function wrap(g,text,w){const out=[];let line='';text.split(' ').forEach(t=>{const n=line?line+' '+t:t;if(g.measureText(n).width>w&&line){out.push(line);line=t}else line=n});if(line)out.push(line);return out}

class ZMotion{
 constructor(out,size){this.out=out;this.S=size||640;out.width=out.height=this.S;this.g=out.getContext('2d');
  this.gl=document.createElement('canvas');this.gl.width=this.gl.height=this.S;
  const gl=this.GL=this.gl.getContext('webgl',{preserveDrawingBuffer:true,antialias:false,alpha:false});if(!gl)throw new Error('NO_WEBGL');
  const mk=(t,s)=>{const o=gl.createShader(t);gl.shaderSource(o,s);gl.compileShader(o);if(!gl.getShaderParameter(o,gl.COMPILE_STATUS))throw new Error(gl.getShaderInfoLog(o));return o};
  const pr=this.pr=gl.createProgram();gl.attachShader(pr,mk(gl.VERTEX_SHADER,VS));gl.attachShader(pr,mk(gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER,FS));gl.linkProgram(pr);
  if(!gl.getProgramParameter(pr,gl.LINK_STATUS))throw new Error(gl.getProgramInfoLog(pr));gl.useProgram(pr);
  gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,gl.createBuffer());gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,new Float32Array([-1,-1,3,-1,-1,3]),gl.STATIC_DRAW);
  const al=gl.getAttribLocation(pr,'a');gl.enableVertexAttribArray(al);gl.vertexAttribPointer(al,2,gl.FLOAT,false,0,0);
  this.U={};'uImg uBlur uDepth uR uT uP uS uI uFade uF uFR uAcc'.split(' ').forEach(n=>this.U[n]=gl.getUniformLocation(pr,n));
  gl.viewport(0,0,this.S,this.S);}
 tex(unit,src){const gl=this.GL;const t=gl.createTexture();gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE0+unit);gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D,t);
  gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_FLIP_Y_WEBGL,true);gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D,0,gl.RGBA,gl.RGBA,gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE,src);
  gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER,gl.LINEAR);gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER,gl.LINEAR);
  gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_S,gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_T,gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);return t}
 /* img: loaded <img>/<canvas>/ImageBitmap. The picture is cropped to a square (centre, or around the focus). */
 setImage(img,plan){
  plan=this.plan=Object.assign({focus:{x:.5,y:.5,r:.25},accent:'#ffcf7a',intensity:1,pitch:null},plan||{});
  const w=img.naturalWidth||img.width,h=img.naturalHeight||img.height,s=Math.min(w,h),F=plan.focus;
  let sx=Math.max(0,Math.min(w-s,F.x*w-s/2)),sy=Math.max(0,Math.min(h-s,F.y*h-s/2));
  const N=Math.min(1024,s),c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=N;c.getContext('2d').drawImage(img,sx,sy,s,s,0,0,N,N);
  // the focus, now measured inside the square crop
  this.f={x:(F.x*w-sx)/s,y:(F.y*h-sy)/s,r:F.r};
  // soft copy (for glow and depth-of-field)
  const b=document.createElement('canvas');b.width=b.height=96;const bg=b.getContext('2d');bg.drawImage(c,0,0,96,96);
  const b2=document.createElement('canvas');b2.width=b2.height=256;const g2=b2.getContext('2d');try{g2.filter='blur(3px)'}catch(e){}g2.drawImage(b,0,0,256,256);
  // estimated depth: near the focus, in brighter and in lower parts = closer. An estimate, not a measurement.
  const D=128,dc=document.createElement('canvas');dc.width=dc.height=D;const dg=dc.getContext('2d');dg.drawImage(c,0,0,D,D);
  const px=dg.getImageData(0,0,D,D),d=px.data;const lum=new Float32Array(D*D);for(let i=0;i<D*D;i++)lum[i]=(d[i*4]*.3+d[i*4+1]*.59+d[i*4+2]*.11)/255;
  const raw=new Float32Array(D*D);
  for(let y=0;y<D;y++)for(let x=0;x<D;x++){const dx=x/D-this.f.x,dy=y/D-this.f.y,r=Math.hypot(dx,dy),fo=Math.exp(-(r*r)/(2*Math.pow(Math.max(.08,this.f.r),2)));
   raw[y*D+x]=.5*fo+.3*lum[y*D+x]+.2*(y/D);}
  const bl=new Float32Array(D*D);for(let y=0;y<D;y++)for(let x=0;x<D;x++){let s2=0,n=0;for(let k=-3;k<=3;k++)for(let m=-3;m<=3;m++){const yy=y+k,xx=x+m;if(yy>=0&&yy<D&&xx>=0&&xx<D){s2+=raw[yy*D+xx];n++}}bl[y*D+x]=s2/n}
  let mn=1,mx=0;bl.forEach(v=>{mn=Math.min(mn,v);mx=Math.max(mx,v)});
  for(let i=0;i<D*D;i++){const v=Math.round(255*(bl[i]-mn)/(mx-mn||1));d[i*4]=d[i*4+1]=d[i*4+2]=v;d[i*4+3]=255}
  dg.putImageData(px,0,0);
  const gl=this.GL;this.tImg=this.tex(0,c);this.tBlur=this.tex(1,b2);this.tDepth=this.tex(2,dc);
  gl.uniform1i(this.U.uImg,0);gl.uniform1i(this.U.uBlur,1);gl.uniform1i(this.U.uDepth,2);this.ready=true}
 /* draw one frame. style: '2d'|'3d'|'simulation'|'pitch'; t seconds; dur seconds */
 draw(style,t,dur){
  if(!this.ready)return;const gl=this.GL,U=this.U,S=this.S,P=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t/dur));
  const fade=Math.min(1,t/.45,(dur-t)/.45);
  gl.uniform2f(U.uR,S,S);gl.uniform1f(U.uT,t);gl.uniform1f(U.uP,P);gl.uniform1f(U.uS,STYLES[style]);gl.uniform1f(U.uI,this.plan.intensity);
  gl.uniform1f(U.uFade,Math.max(0,fade));gl.uniform2f(U.uF,this.f.x,this.f.y);gl.uniform1f(U.uFR,this.f.r);gl.uniform3fv(U.uAcc,hex(this.plan.accent));
  gl.drawArrays(gl.TRIANGLES,0,3);
  const g=this.g;g.drawImage(this.gl,0,0,S,S);
  const k=S/640,al=Math.max(0,fade);
  if(style==='pitch'&&this.plan.pitch)this.pitch(g,t,dur,k,al);
  g.globalAlpha=.7*al;g.fillStyle='#fff';g.font=(11*k)+'px system-ui,sans-serif';g.textAlign='left';
  g.fillText('ZULORA · MOTION PREVIEW · '+LABEL[style].toUpperCase(),16*k,24*k);g.globalAlpha=1}
 pitch(g,t,dur,k,al){
  const p=this.plan.pitch,acc=this.plan.accent,x0=36*k,w=this.S-72*k;let y=this.S-196*k;
  const A=(t0)=>Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(t-t0)/.7))*al;
  g.textAlign='left';g.textBaseline='alphabetic';
  g.globalAlpha=A(.6);g.fillStyle=acc;g.fillRect(x0,y-34*k,46*k,3*k);
  g.fillStyle='#fff';g.font='bold '+(34*k)+'px Georgia,serif';let l=wrap(g,p.title||'',w);l.forEach((s,i)=>g.fillText(s,x0,y+i*38*k));y+=l.length*38*k+8*k;
  g.font=(17*k)+'px system-ui,sans-serif';
  (p.points||[]).slice(0,3).forEach((s,i)=>{const a=A(1.8+i*1.5);g.globalAlpha=a;g.fillStyle=acc;g.beginPath();g.arc(x0+5*k,y-5*k,3.5*k,0,6.3);g.fill();
   g.fillStyle='#fff';wrap(g,s,w-24*k).forEach((q,j)=>{g.fillText(q,x0+20*k,y+j*22*k)});y+=(wrap(g,s,w-24*k).length)*22*k+6*k});
  if(p.tagline){g.globalAlpha=A(dur-2.3);g.fillStyle=acc;g.font='italic '+(18*k)+'px Georgia,serif';g.fillText(p.tagline,x0,this.S-24*k)}
  g.globalAlpha=1}
 /* live playback (loops). returns a stop function */
 play(style,dur,loop){let st=0,id=0,stop=false;const f=now=>{if(stop)return;if(!st)st=now;let t=(now-st)/1000;if(t>dur){if(loop){st=now;t=0}else{return}}this.draw(style,t,dur);id=requestAnimationFrame(f)};id=requestAnimationFrame(f);return()=>{stop=true;cancelAnimationFrame(id)}}
 /* record to a file (WebM) so the creator can download it */
 record(style,dur,fps){return new Promise((res,rej)=>{if(!this.out.captureStream||typeof MediaRecorder==='undefined')return rej(new Error('NO_RECORDER'));
  const mime=['video/webm;codecs=vp9','video/webm;codecs=vp8','video/webm'].find(m=>MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(m));if(!mime)return rej(new Error('NO_RECORDER'));
  const rec=new MediaRecorder(this.out.captureStream(fps||24),{mimeType:mime,videoBitsPerSecond:2500000}),ch=[];rec.ondataavailable=e=>e.data.size&&ch.push(e.data);
  rec.onstop=()=>{cancel();res(new Blob(ch,{type:'video/webm'}))};rec.start();const t0=performance.now();let raf;
  const cancel=()=>cancelAnimationFrame(raf);const f=now=>{const t=(now-t0)/1000;if(t>=dur){rec.stop();return}this.draw(style,t,dur);raf=requestAnimationFrame(f)};raf=requestAnimationFrame(f)})}
}
root.ZMotion=ZMotion;
})(window);

/* Zulora "Bring it to life" panel. Uses ZMotion (above). env = { call(body,ms)->Promise<{data}>, err(e)->Promise<string>, rm:boolean }.
   Two kinds of result: (1) motion previews drawn on the phone (2D, 3D, simulation, pitch) and (2) an "AI video" that NVIDIA Cosmos 3 Nano generates (from the saved picture, or from words).
   Everything the AI returns is drawn onto a canvas as plain text or played as a video, never inserted as HTML. */
(function(root){
const ST=[['2d','2D','A cinematic camera moves toward the heart of your picture.'],['3d','3D','The camera glides around your picture. Depth is estimated, so it is a convincing illusion, not a real 3D model.'],['simulation','Simulation','Flow, rings and drifting light over your picture. Illustrative only, not real physics.'],['pitch','Pitch','Your idea as a short pitch with captions. The words are AI-written suggestions.']];
const AI=['ai','AI video'],UF=['uf','Unfold'];
const DUR=8,SIZE=512;
function ZMO(env){
 let el,eng,cv,stopPlay=null,S=null;const PL={};
 const esc=s=>String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
 function build(){if(el)return;el=document.createElement('div');el.id='mo';el.hidden=true;el.setAttribute('role','dialog');el.setAttribute('aria-modal','true');el.setAttribute('aria-label','Bring your picture to life');document.body.appendChild(el);
  el.addEventListener('click',onClick);el.addEventListener('input',e=>{if(!S||!e.target)return;if(e.target.id==='mop')S.vp[S.vmode]=e.target.value.slice(0,500);else if(e.target.id==='ufg'&&S.uf)S.uf.grow=e.target.value.slice(0,300)});document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&!el.hidden)close()})}
 function halt(){if(stopPlay){stopPlay();stopPlay=null}}
 function freeVideo(){if(S&&S.vurl){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(S.vurl)}catch(_){}S.vurl=''}}
 let freeUFref=()=>{};
 function close(){halt();freeVideo();freeUFref();if(el)el.hidden=true;S=null;document.body.classList.remove('mo-open')}
 function open(o){build();halt();freeVideo();freeUFref();const sty=['2d','3d','simulation','pitch'].includes(o.style)?o.style:'3d';S={uf:null,id:o.imageId,ideaId:o.ideaId||'',url:o.url,title:o.title||'',eng:null,style:sty,vstyle:sty,dirbusy:false,used:'',vmode:'image2video',vp:{image2video:'',text2video:''},made:false,sel:[sty],stage:'make',msg:'',err:'',tab:'ai',saving:false,pvbusy:false,plan:PL[o.imageId]||null,ai:'idle',vurl:'',vblob:null};S.uf=ufNew();
  el.hidden=false;document.body.classList.add('mo-open');view();aiinfo(S);const b=el.querySelector('.moc');if(b)b.focus()}
 const VD={'2d':'Flat, graphic animation. Elements flow and pulse in place, with no camera depth.','3d':'The camera moves around your picture and shows its depth and volume.','simulation':'Your idea shown working: parts moving, energy and flow, with a steady camera.','pitch':'A cinematic reveal with transitions and sweeping light, made to present your idea.'};
 function paintLens(){const m=el&&el.querySelector('#mol');if(m&&S)m.innerHTML=env.lensBlock(S.ideaId,S.style)}
 function view(){if(!S)return;halt();

 function lastFrame(blob){return new Promise((res,rej)=>{const u=URL.createObjectURL(blob),v=document.createElement('video');v.muted=true;v.playsInline=true;v.preload='auto';const done=(x,e)=>{try{URL.revokeObjectURL(u)}catch(_){}v.removeAttribute('src');e?rej(e):res(x)};
   const t=setTimeout(()=>done(null,new Error('FRAME')),15000);
   v.onerror=()=>{clearTimeout(t);done(null,new Error('FRAME'))};
   v.onloadedmetadata=()=>{try{v.currentTime=Math.max(0,(v.duration||1)-.08)}catch(e){clearTimeout(t);done(null,e)}};
   v.onseeked=()=>{clearTimeout(t);try{const w=v.videoWidth,h=v.videoHeight,k=Math.min(1,640/Math.max(w,h)),c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=Math.round(w*k);c.height=Math.round(h*k);c.getContext('2d').drawImage(v,0,0,c.width,c.height);let q=.82,s=c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',q);while(s.length>240000&&q>.4){q-=.14;s=c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',q)}done(s)}catch(e){done(null,e)}};
   v.src=u})}
 async function ufMake(i){const sid=S,U=sid.uf,c=U.ch[i];if(!c||c.state==='gen')return false;c.state='gen';c.err='';U.err='';U.busy=true;ufPaint();
  let ok=false;
  try{let frame;if(i>0&&U.ch[i-1].blob){try{frame=await lastFrame(U.ch[i-1].blob)}catch(_){frame=undefined}}   // continue from where the last chapter ended; falls back to your picture
   const body={action:'video',mode:'image2video',image_id:sid.id,prompt:c.direction};if(frame)body.frame=frame;
   const r=await env.call(body,150000),b64=r.data&&r.data.video;if(S!==sid)return false;
   if(typeof b64!=='string'||b64.length<1000)throw new Error('EMPTY');
   const bin=atob(b64),u8=new Uint8Array(bin.length);for(let k=0;k<bin.length;k++)u8[k]=bin.charCodeAt(k);
   c.blob=new Blob([u8],{type:'video/mp4'});c.url=URL.createObjectURL(c.blob);c.state='ready';ok=true;U.msg='';
   if(U.ch.filter(x=>x.state==='ready').length===1)ufShow(i,true)}
  catch(e){if(S!==sid)return false;c.state='fail';c.err=await env.err(e)}
  if(S===sid){U.busy=false;ufPaint()}
  return ok}

   U.ch.push({title:String(c.title||'').slice(0,40),caption:String(c.caption||'').slice(0,260),direction:String(c.direction||'').slice(0,450),state:'wait',err:'',blob:null,url:''});U.grow='';U.busy=false;
   const ta=el.querySelector('#ufg');if(ta)ta.value='';ufPaint();await ufMake(U.ch.length-1)}
  catch(e){if(S!==sid)return;U.busy=false;U.err=await env.err(e);ufPaint()}}
 function ufSave(i){const c=S.uf.ch[i];if(!c||!c.blob)return;const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=c.url;a.download='zulora-chapter-'+(i+1)+'-'+new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)+'.mp4';document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove()}

  const first=U.ch.findIndex(c=>c.state==='ready');if(first<0)return;const atEnd=U.cur>=0&&U.ch[U.cur]&&v.ended;ufShow(atEnd||U.cur<0?first:U.cur,true)}
 function onClick(e){const b=e.target.closest('[data-m]');if(!b||!S)return;const m=b.dataset.m;
  if(m==='close')close();
  else if(m==='tog'){const s=b.dataset.s,i=S.sel.indexOf(s);if(i>=0)S.sel.splice(i,1);else if(S.sel.length<3)S.sel.push(s);else{S.msg='';S.err='Up to three. Turn one off first.';view();return}S.err='';view()}

  else if(m==='lens')env.lensRun(S.ideaId,S.style,paintLens)
  else if(m==='save')save();
  else if(m==='aigo')aigo();
  else if(m==='aichk')aichk();
  else if(m==='aidir')aidir();

  else if(m==='ufplan')ufPlan();
  else if(m==='ufmake')ufMake(+b.dataset.s);
  else if(m==='ufall')ufAll();
  else if(m==='ufgrow')ufGrow();
  else if(m==='ufshow')ufShow(+b.dataset.s,true);
  else if(m==='ufsave')ufSave(+b.dataset.s);
  else if(m==='ufplay')ufPlay();
  else if(m==='aireset'){S.ai='idle';S.err='';S.msg='';view()}
  else if(m==='vs'){S.vstyle=b.dataset.s;S.err='';S.msg='';view()}
  else if(m==='aiopen'){S.stage='play';S.tab='ai';S.err='';S.msg='';view();if(!S.eng)aiinfo(S)}
  else if(m==='vm'){S.vmode=b.dataset.s;S.err='';S.msg='';if(S.ai==='fail')S.ai='idle';view()}
  else if(m==='aisave')aisave();
  else if(m==='play'){S.paused=false;S.started=1;view()}
  else if(m==='back'){S.stage='choose';S.msg='';S.err='';view()}}
 freeUFref=freeUF;
 return{open,close}}
root.ZMO=ZMO;
})(window);

<script>
/* ZULORA cosmic opening v2. One fragment shader draws everything: procedural nebula + starfield, a perspective-tilted
   procedural spiral galaxy, ray-traced sphere planets with an animated Sun, and a procedural Earth. Single rAF loop. */
(()=>{
const FS=`precision highp float;
uniform vec2 uR;uniform vec3 uRo,uTa;uniform float uA,uOff,uT,uAg,uAs,uK,uGrow,uSpark,uLife,uNeb,uStar,uGR;
const float F=1.5;
float h21(vec2 p){p=fract(p*vec2(123.34,456.21));p+=dot(p,p+45.32);return fract(p.x*p.y);}
float n2(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);return mix(mix(h21(i),h21(i+vec2(1,0)),f.x),mix(h21(i+vec2(0,1)),h21(i+vec2(1,1)),f.x),f.y);}
float f2(vec2 p){float s=0.,a=.5;for(int i=0;i<5;i++){s+=a*n2(p);p=p*2.03+vec2(7.1,3.7);a*=.5;}return s;}
float h31(vec3 p){p=fract(p*.3183099+.1);p*=17.;return fract(p.x*p.y*p.z*(p.x+p.y+p.z));}
float n3(vec3 x){vec3 i=floor(x),f=fract(x);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);return mix(mix(mix(h31(i),h31(i+vec3(1,0,0)),f.x),mix(h31(i+vec3(0,1,0)),h31(i+vec3(1,1,0)),f.x),f.y),mix(mix(h31(i+vec3(0,0,1)),h31(i+vec3(1,0,1)),f.x),mix(h31(i+vec3(0,1,1)),h31(i+vec3(1,1,1)),f.x),f.y),f.z);}
float f3(vec3 p){float s=0.,a=.5;for(int i=0;i<6;i++){s+=a*n3(p);p=p*2.02+vec3(3.1,1.7,5.3);a*=.5;}return s;}
float f3c(vec3 p){float s=0.,a=.5;for(int i=0;i<4;i++){s+=a*n3(p);p=p*2.02+vec3(3.1,1.7,5.3);a*=.5;}return s/.9375;}
vec4 PD(int i){if(i==0)return vec4(1.92,.17,1.6,0.);if(i==1)return vec4(3.,.29,1.2,0.);if(i==2)return vec4(4.08,.36,1.,0.);if(i==3)return vec4(5.28,.24,.8,0.);if(i==4)return vec4(7.2,.66,.45,0.);if(i==5)return vec4(8.88,.56,.33,0.);return vec4(10.32,.43,.24,0.);}
vec3 PC(int i){if(i==0)return vec3(.72,.65,.56);if(i==1)return vec3(.91,.79,.54);if(i==2)return vec3(.24,.48,.88);if(i==3)return vec3(.85,.41,.29);if(i==4)return vec3(.85,.72,.55);if(i==5)return vec3(.9,.82,.63);return vec3(.5,.82,.88);}
vec3 stars(vec3 d){vec3 c=vec3(0);float band=exp(-pow(dot(d,vec3(.3,.8,.5))*3.5,2.));
 for(int l=0;l<3;l++){float sc=38.+float(l)*52.;vec3 q=d*sc+vec3(float(l)*3.7);vec3 id=floor(q),f=fract(q)-.5;float h=h31(id);
 if(h>.82-.12*band*float(l)){vec3 o=(vec3(h31(id+1.3),h31(id+5.7),h31(id+9.1))-.5)*.6;vec3 e=f-o;float tw=.65+.35*sin(uA*(1.+h*3.)+h*60.);
  float k=h31(id+4.);vec3 tint=k<.2?vec3(1.,.55,.4):(k<.5?vec3(.6,.75,1.):(k<.6?vec3(1.,.6,.85):vec3(1.,.95,.85)));
  float b=max(h-.76,0.)*9.;c+=(exp(-dot(e,e)*(110.+float(l)*60.))*b+(h>.965?exp(-dot(e,e)*14.)*.35:0.))*tw*tint;}}return c;}
vec3 neb(vec3 d){float t=uA*.02;vec3 q=d*2.2+vec3(t,0.,-t*.6);float a=f3c(q),b=f3c(q*2.1+a*1.8+5.);float m=smoothstep(.3,.78,a*.55+b*.6);
 vec3 c=(mix(vec3(.06,.02,.2),vec3(.42,.13,.6),m)+vec3(0.,.2,.32)*smoothstep(.45,.9,b))*m*(.45+.55*a);
 float band=exp(-pow(dot(d,vec3(.3,.8,.5))*3.5,2.));float w=f3c(d*4.+vec3(t*.5,3.,0.));float lane=smoothstep(.48,.68,f3c(d*7.+2.));
 c+=mix(vec3(.55,.42,.32),vec3(.3,.4,.6),f3c(d*5.+9.))*band*smoothstep(.3,.8,w)*(1.-.75*lane)*.55;return c;}
/* spiral galaxy: magenta/violet arms, blue rim, red star-forming knots, dust lanes, warm core with a dark ring */
vec3 galaxy(vec2 q){float r=length(q);if(r>1.7)return vec3(0);
 float a=atan(q.y,q.x)-uA*.05/(r+.3);
 float ph=2.*(a+4.6*r)+2.4*(f2(q*6.+3.)-.5);
 float arm=pow(.5+.5*cos(ph),1.4),outer=exp(-r*1.8),core=exp(-r*r*16.);
 float dust=smoothstep(.5,.72,f2(q*15.))*smoothstep(.12,.35,r);
 float lane=smoothstep(.5,.8,.5+.5*cos(ph-.9))*smoothstep(.12,.4,r);
 float den=arm*outer*(.45+f2(q*11.))*2.*(1.-.75*max(dust,lane*.85))+core*1.8;
 den*=1.-.35*smoothstep(.1,.16,r)*(1.-smoothstep(.16,.24,r));
 vec3 base=mix(vec3(1.,.8,.66),mix(vec3(.85,.3,.95),vec3(.28,.5,1.),smoothstep(.3,1.,r)),smoothstep(.05,.42,r));
 vec3 c=base*den;
 c+=vec3(1.,.2,.4)*smoothstep(.78,.95,h21(floor(q*75.)))*arm*smoothstep(.22,.5,r)*(1.-smoothstep(.9,1.3,r))*1.4;
 c+=vec3(.85,.9,1.)*smoothstep(.85,1.,h21(floor(q*150.+7.)))*den*.9;
 return c*smoothstep(1.7,.75,r);}
float sph(vec3 ro,vec3 rd,vec3 c,float r){vec3 oc=ro-c;float b=dot(oc,rd),h=b*b-dot(oc,oc)+r*r;if(h<0.)return -1.;float t=-b-sqrt(h);return t>0.?t:-1.;}
vec3 earthShade(vec3 n,vec3 L,vec3 V){
 float a=uT*.12,ca=cos(a),sa=sin(a);vec3 pn=vec3(ca*n.x+sa*n.z,n.y,-sa*n.x+ca*n.z);
 float h=f3(pn*2.1+3.),land=smoothstep(.5,.52,h),lat=abs(pn.y);
 vec3 ocean=mix(vec3(.004,.03,.13),vec3(.03,.2,.42),smoothstep(.2,.46,h));
 float des=smoothstep(.5,.75,f3c(pn*2.6+30.)+(1.-smoothstep(.1,.45,abs(lat-.35)))*.4);
 vec3 ter=mix(mix(vec3(.04,.17,.05),vec3(.17,.3,.1),f3c(pn*8.)),vec3(.58,.44,.25),des);
 ter=mix(ter,vec3(.36,.31,.27),smoothstep(.57,.75,h));
 float ice=smoothstep(.78,.9,lat+(h-.5)*.3);
 vec3 alb=mix(mix(ocean,ter,land),vec3(.93,.96,1.),ice);
 float cl=smoothstep(.5,.78,f3(pn*2.8+vec3(uT*.01,0.,0.)+9.+(f3c(pn*1.5+3.)-.5)*.9));
 float nl=dot(n,L),dif=max(nl,0.)*smoothstep(-.05,.12,nl);
 vec3 c=mix(alb,vec3(1.),cl*.92)*(.03+dif*1.25);
 c+=vec3(.9,.95,1.)*pow(max(dot(n,normalize(L+V)),0.),90.)*(1.-land)*(1.-cl)*(1.-ice)*1.1*step(0.,nl);
 float night=smoothstep(.12,-.2,nl);
 c+=vec3(1.,.68,.32)*smoothstep(.5,.7,f3c(pn*14.+1.))*land*(1.-ice)*(1.-cl*.85)*night*smoothstep(.53,.58,h)*uLife*3.2;
 c+=vec3(.9,.4,.15)*pow(1.-abs(nl),7.)*.1*(1.-cl*.5);
 c+=vec3(.25,.5,1.)*pow(1.-max(dot(n,V),0.),3.)*(.08+dif*1.2);
 return c;}
vec3 planetShade(vec3 n,vec3 L,vec3 col,float bands,float sd,vec3 V){
 float gas=step(10.,bands);float b=sin(n.y*bands+f3c(n*3.+sd)*4.)*.5+.5;
 vec3 a=mix(col*(.5+.6*f3c(n*9.+sd)),mix(col*.5,col*1.3+vec3(.12),b),gas);
 float dif=max(dot(n,L),0.);return a*(.02+dif*1.3)+vec3(.4,.55,1.)*pow(1.-max(dot(n,V),0.),4.)*.15*dif;}
void main(){
 vec2 p=(gl_FragCoord.xy-.5*uR)/uR.y-vec2(0.,uOff);
 vec3 ro=uRo,fw=normalize(uTa-ro),rt=normalize(cross(fw,vec3(0,1,0))),up=cross(rt,fw),rd=normalize(p.x*rt+p.y*up+F*fw);
 vec3 col=neb(rd)*uNeb*.8+stars(rd)*uStar;
 if(uAg>0.&&rd.y*ro.y<0.){float t=-ro.y/rd.y;col+=galaxy((ro+rd*t).xz/uGR)*uAg;}
 float tH=1e9;int hid=-1;vec3 hC=vec3(0),E=vec3(0),C5=vec3(0);float fo=1.-smoothstep(.3,.8,uK);
 if(uAs>0.){
  for(int i=0;i<7;i++){vec4 d=PD(i);float an=uT*d.z*.18+float(i)*1.9,ob=d.x*uGrow;vec3 C=vec3(cos(an)*ob,0.,sin(an)*ob);
   if(i==2)E=C;if(i==5)C5=C;float t=sph(ro,rd,C,d.y);if(t>0.&&t<tH){tH=t;hid=i;hC=C;}}
  float ts=sph(ro,rd,vec3(0),.7);if(ts>0.&&ts<tH){tH=ts;hid=7;}
  if(hid>=0){vec3 P=ro+rd*tH,n=normalize(P-hC),L=normalize(-P),c;
   if(hid==7){vec3 nn=normalize(P);float g1=f3c(nn*7.+uA*.06),g2=f3c(nn*18.-uA*.1),mu=max(dot(nn,-rd),0.);
    c=mix(vec3(.9,.18,.02),vec3(1.,.75,.2),smoothstep(.25,.75,g1))*(2.2+2.*g2)*(.55+.6*mu)*(1.-.55*smoothstep(.62,.7,f3c(nn*4.+3.)))+vec3(1.,.9,.6)*pow(mu,3.)*1.2;}
   else if(hid==2)c=earthShade(n,L,-rd);
   else c=planetShade(n,L,PC(hid),(hid>=4)?22.:3.,float(hid)*3.1,-rd);
   col=mix(col,c,uAs*((hid==2||hid==7)?1.:fo));}
  vec3 nr=normalize(vec3(.25,1.,.1));float dn=dot(rd,nr);
  if(abs(dn)>.001){float tr=dot(C5-ro,nr)/dn;if(tr>0.&&tr<tH){float rr=length(ro+rd*tr-C5)/.56;
   col=mix(col,vec3(.85,.75,.55)*.8,smoothstep(1.35,1.45,rr)*smoothstep(2.3,2.1,rr)*(.35+.4*sin(rr*40.))*.8*uAs);}}
  if(rd.y*ro.y<0.){float t=-ro.y/rd.y;if(t<tH){vec3 hp=ro+rd*t;float r=length(hp.xz),w=t/(F*uR.y);
   for(int i=0;i<7;i++){float e=abs(r-PD(i).x*uGrow);col+=vec3(.55,.65,.95)*smoothstep(w*1.6,0.,e)*.34*uAs*fo;}
   float ba=atan(hp.z,hp.x),bm=1.-smoothstep(0.,.55,abs(r-5.28*uGrow*1.18));col+=vec3(.75,.68,.6)*smoothstep(.82,.96,h21(floor(vec2(ba*90.,r*16.))))*bm*uAs*fo*1.5;}}
  float Rr=.36,Ra=Rr*1.09;vec3 oc=E-ro;float tc=dot(oc,rd);float dm=length(oc-rd*tc);
  if(tc>0.&&dm<Ra){float ch=sqrt(Ra*Ra-dm*dm);if(dm<Rr)ch-=sqrt(Rr*Rr-dm*dm);else ch*=2.;
   vec3 nn=normalize(ro+rd*tc-E),Ls=normalize(-E);float d=dot(nn,Ls);
   col+=mix(vec3(.2,.45,1.),vec3(1.,.45,.2),exp(-d*d*14.)*.65)*(1.-exp(-ch/(Rr*.05)))*smoothstep(-.35,.6,d)*1.1*uAs;}
 }
 /* sun / spark glow with lens streaks */
 if(hid<0){vec3 v=-ro;float z=dot(v,fw);if(z>0.){vec2 sp=F*vec2(dot(v,rt),dot(v,up))/z,d=p-sp;float s=length(d);
  vec3 g=vec3(1.,.66,.28)*(.03/(s+.03)*.6+exp(-s*s*900.)*1.1);
  g+=vec3(1.,.7,.4)*(exp(-abs(d.y)*220.)*exp(-abs(d.x)*9.)+exp(-abs(d.x)*220.)*exp(-abs(d.y)*9.))*.22*uAs*fo;
  col+=g*(uSpark+uAg*.9+uAs*fo*.9);}}

 gl_FragColor=vec4(max(col,0.),1.);}`;
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);let cv=$('c');

const STAGES=[['I · Void','Humanity cannot build what humanity has never imagined.'],['II · Universe','An idea begins as a single point of possibility.'],['III · Solar System','Possibilities find structure, relationships, direction.'],['IV · Life','Where Humanity Explores What Comes Next.']];
const CYC=48,PO=[1.92,3,4.08],V={add:(a,b)=>a.map((x,i)=>x+b[i]),mul:(a,s)=>a.map(x=>x*s),nrm:a=>{const l=Math.hypot(...a)||1;return a.map(x=>x/l)},
 crs:(a,b)=>[a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1],a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2],a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0]],mix:(a,b,t)=>a.map((x,i)=>x+(b[i]-x)*t)};

function glInit(){try{
 GL=cv.getContext('webgl',{antialias:false,alpha:false,powerPreference:'high-performance'});if(!GL)return false;

function glDraw(){

 const r1=[Math.cos(az)*Math.cos(el)*dist,Math.sin(el)*dist,Math.sin(az)*Math.cos(el)*dist];
 const d=.36*Math.sqrt(1+(1.5/rad)**2),sv=V.nrm(V.mul(E,-1)),side=V.nrm(V.crs([0,1,0],sv)),ang=1.05;
 const c=V.nrm(V.add(V.add(V.mul(sv,Math.cos(ang)),V.mul(side,Math.sin(ang))),[0,.25,0])),r2=V.add(E,V.mul(c,d));

 u('uNeb',.2+.8*Math.max((1-sm(T,12,20))*(.5+.5*sm(T,0,5)),sm(T,44,48)*.5));u('uStar',.9-.4*sm(T,28,31)+.2*sm(T,31,34)+.2*sm(T,42,48));

function geoLife(){const r=rg(11),seg=[];const br=(x,y,a,len,d,b0)=>{const x1=x+Math.cos(a)*len,y1=y+Math.sin(a)*len*.78;seg.push([x,y,x1,y1,d,b0]);if(d>=4)return;const k=d<1?3:2;for(let i=0;i<k;i++){const da=(i-(k-1)/2)*(.55+r()*.3)+(r()-.5)*.25;br(x1,y1,a+da,len*(.68+r()*.1),d+1,b0+.13+r()*.04)}};

function geoTech(){const r=rg(5),N=[[0,0,0]];for(let i=1;i<70;i++){const a=r()*6.283,d=Math.sqrt(r())*.95+.08;N.push([Math.cos(a)*d,Math.sin(a)*d*.85,d*.85])}

  E.push([bi[0],i]);if(bi[1]>=0&&bd[1]<.45&&r()<.55)E.push([bi[1],i])}
 return{N,E}}

const GXF=`
#ifdef GL_FRAGMENT_PRECISION_HIGH
precision highp float;
#else
precision mediump float;
#endif
uniform vec2 R;uniform float T,P,W;
float h12(vec2 p){vec3 q=fract(vec3(p.xyx)*.1031);q+=dot(q,q.yzx+33.33);return fract((q.x+q.y)*q.z);}
float nz(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);return mix(mix(h12(i),h12(i+vec2(1.,0.)),f.x),mix(h12(i+vec2(0.,1.)),h12(i+vec2(1.,1.)),f.x),f.y);}
float fbm(vec2 p){float v=0.,a=.5;mat2 m=mat2(1.6,1.2,-1.2,1.6);for(int i=0;i<4;i++){v+=a*nz(p);p=m*p;a*=.5;}return v;}
vec3 pal(float p,int k){
  vec3 a=vec3(.05,.05,.20),b=vec3(.07,.24,.45),c=vec3(.27,.14,.26),d=vec3(.06,.20,.42);
  vec3 u=vec3(.34,.16,.62),v=vec3(.12,.46,.62),w=vec3(.55,.34,.52),x=vec3(.24,.52,.86);
  vec3 base=mix(mix(mix(a,b,smoothstep(.1,.4,p)),c,smoothstep(.45,.68,p)),d,smoothstep(.78,1.,p));
  vec3 hi=mix(mix(mix(u,v,smoothstep(.1,.4,p)),w,smoothstep(.45,.68,p)),x,smoothstep(.78,1.,p));
  return k==0?base:hi;
}
void main(){
  vec2 uv=(gl_FragCoord.xy-.5*R)/R.y;           // y in -.5..+.5, centre 0
  vec2 C=vec2(0.,.2);                            // focal point, kept in the upper part so text stays readable
  vec2 p=uv-C;float r=length(p);
  float t=T;
  float w0=1.-smoothstep(0.,.38,P);
  float w1=smoothstep(.05,.34,P)*(1.-smoothstep(.45,.68,P));
  float w2=smoothstep(.4,.68,P)*(1.-smoothstep(.82,1.,P));
  float w3=smoothstep(.76,1.,P);
  /* nebula: two warped noise fields drifting at different speeds */
  vec2 q=uv*1.5+vec2(t*.021,-t*.013);
  vec2 wv=vec2(fbm(q*1.2+vec2(0.,t*.03)),fbm(q*1.2+vec2(5.2,1.3-t*.026)));
  float n=fbm(q+wv*2.4+vec2(t*.01,0.));
  vec3 base=pal(P,0),hi=pal(P,1);
  vec3 col=base*(.35+.9*n);
  col+=hi*pow(n,3.)*1.5*smoothstep(.1,.9,wv.x);
  /* seed of light with expanding shock rings */
  float pulse=1.+.14*sin(t*1.9);
  float core=.018/(r+.018)*pulse;
  float ring=0.;
  for(int k=0;k<3;k++){float f=fract(t*.09+float(k)/3.);float rr=f*.85;ring+=exp(-pow((r-rr)*16.,2.))*(1.-f)*(1.-f);}
  col+=w0*(vec3(1.,.78,.45)*core*1.05+vec3(.55,.5,1.)*ring*.55);
  /* spiral galaxy: arms wind and slowly turn, with a warm bulge */
  float a=atan(p.y,p.x);
  float arm=sin(2.*a-6.2*log(r+.04)+t*.22)*.5+.5;
  float arm2=sin(2.*a-6.2*log(r+.04)+t*.22+.7)*.5+.5;arm=(pow(arm,3.2)*.9+pow(arm2,5.)*.5)*exp(-r*3.4)*(.45+1.1*fbm(vec2(a*3.,log(r+.04)*6.-t*.05)));
  float dust=smoothstep(.78,.97,nz(p*95.+vec2(t*.4,0.)))*exp(-r*3.)*(.3+arm*2.);
  float bulge=.04/(r+.05);
  col+=w1*(hi*arm*2.4+vec3(1.,.8,.55)*bulge*.55+vec3(.6,.75,1.)*arm*.5+vec3(.9,.95,1.)*dust*1.2);
  /* star system: glow + planets on slanted orbits, always circling */
  vec3 sys=vec3(0.);
  float gl=.03/(r+.03);sys+=vec3(1.,.72,.42)*gl*.9;
  for(int i=0;i<3;i++){
    float fi=float(i);float orb=.17+fi*.13;float sp=(.55-fi*.13)*(i==1?-1.:1.);
    float ang=t*sp*.5+fi*2.1;vec2 pc=vec2(cos(ang)*orb,sin(ang)*orb*.42);
    vec2 pp=p-pc*vec2(1.,1.);
    float od=abs(length(vec2(p.x,p.y/.42))-orb)*.42;sys+=vec3(.5,.6,.9)*exp(-od*220.)*.14;
    float rad=.016+fi*.007;float d=length(pp);
    if(d<rad*1.6){vec2 nn=pp/rad;float z=sqrt(max(0.,1.-dot(nn,nn)));vec3 L=normalize(vec3(-pc.x,-pc.y*2.,.55));
      float lit=clamp(dot(vec3(nn,z),L),0.,1.);vec3 pc3=i==0?vec3(.85,.55,.35):i==1?vec3(.4,.65,.95):vec3(.75,.7,.9);
      float disc=smoothstep(rad,rad*.88,d);sys=mix(sys,pc3*(.12+lit*1.1),disc);sys+=pc3*exp(-(d-rad)*90.)*.25*(1.-disc);}
  }
  col+=w2*sys;
  /* a planet rising from below: banded swirling atmosphere, glowing limb */
  vec2 pcen=vec2(0.,-.92);float pr=1.12;vec2 pq=uv-pcen;float pd=length(pq);
  float rim=exp(-abs(pd-pr)*26.)*smoothstep(-.6,.2,uv.y+.2);
  float surf=smoothstep(pr,pr-.012,pd);
  vec2 sp=pq/pr;float bandY=sp.y*5.+fbm(sp*4.+vec2(t*.05,0.))*2.2;
  float cl=fbm(vec2(sp.x*3.2+t*.035,bandY));
  vec3 oc=mix(vec3(.03,.08,.2),vec3(.12,.42,.62),cl)+vec3(.7,.8,.95)*pow(max(0.,fbm(sp*6.+vec2(-t*.05,t*.02))-.5),1.4)*1.4;
  float lightSide=smoothstep(-.7,.5,sp.x*.7+sp.y*.6);
  vec3 pl=oc*(.18+.95*lightSide);
  vec3 atm=vec3(.35,.62,1.)*rim*(.4+.9*lightSide)*1.5;
  col=mix(col,col+atm,w3);
  col=mix(col,pl+atm*.6,w3*surf);
  /* stars flying toward us; during a stage jump they stretch into light-streaks */
  vec2 dir=normalize(uv+vec2(1e-4));vec2 per=vec2(-dir.y,dir.x);
  float spd=.035*(1.+W*18.);
  for(int i=0;i<3;i++){
    float z=fract(t*spd+float(i)/3.);float sc=mix(16.,3.,z);
    vec2 g=uv*sc+vec2(float(i)*17.3,float(i)*9.1);vec2 id=floor(g),lc=fract(g)-.5;
    float hh=h12(id);vec2 off=(vec2(h12(id+7.1),h12(id+3.7))-.5)*.7;
    vec2 d2=lc-off;float al=dot(d2,dir),pe=dot(d2,per);
    float dd=length(vec2(al/(1.+W*9.),pe));
    float fade=smoothstep(0.,.18,z)*smoothstep(1.,.75,z);
    float b=smoothstep(.07+hh*.05,0.,dd)*fade*step(.55,hh);
    b*=.7+.3*sin(t*(2.+hh*5.)+hh*40.);
    col+=mix(vec3(.75,.85,1.),vec3(1.,.88,.7),hh)*b*(.9+W);
  }
  col+=vec3(.5,.6,1.)*W*.08*exp(-r*2.);
  col*=1.-.55*smoothstep(.35,1.1,length(uv));    // soft vignette
  col+=(h12(gl_FragCoord.xy+t)-.5)/255.*2.;      // dither (no banding on dark gradients)
  gl_FragColor=vec4(pow(max(col,0.),vec3(.92)),1.);
}`;
function gxBuild(){
  const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.id='gl';c.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  const g=c.getContext('webgl',{antialias:false,alpha:false,depth:false,stencil:false,powerPreference:'low-power',preserveDrawingBuffer:false});
  if(!g)return 0;
  const sh=(ty,src)=>{const s=g.createShader(ty);g.shaderSource(s,src);g.compileShader(s);if(!g.getShaderParameter(s,g.COMPILE_STATUS)){GX.err=(GX.err||'')+g.getShaderInfoLog(s);return null}return s};
  const vs=sh(g.VERTEX_SHADER,GXV),fs=sh(g.FRAGMENT_SHADER,GXF);if(!vs||!fs)return 0
  const pr=g.createProgram();g.attachShader(pr,vs);g.attachShader(pr,fs);g.linkProgram(pr);if(!g.getProgramParameter(pr,g.LINK_STATUS))return 0;
  g.useProgram(pr);const b=g.createBuffer();g.bindBuffer(g.ARRAY_BUFFER,b);g.bufferData(g.ARRAY_BUFFER,new Float32Array([-1,-1,3,-1,-1,3]),g.STATIC_DRAW);
  const al=g.getAttribLocation(pr,'a');g.enableVertexAttribArray(al);g.vertexAttribPointer(al,2,g.FLOAT,false,0,0);
  GX.u={R:g.getUniformLocation(pr,'R'),T:g.getUniformLocation(pr,'T'),P:g.getUniformLocation(pr,'P'),W:g.getUniformLocation(pr,'W')};
  c.addEventListener('webglcontextlost',e=>{e.preventDefault();GX.ok=-1;vbOff()});
  GX.c=c;GX.g=g;document.body.insertBefore(c,WS);return 1}
function gxSize(){const c=GX.c;if(!c)return;const w=Math.max(2,Math.round(innerWidth*GX.sc)),h=Math.max(2,Math.round(innerHeight*GX.sc));if(c.width!==w||c.height!==h){c.width=w;c.height=h;GX.g.viewport(0,0,w,h)}}
function gxFrame(now){
  GX.raf=requestAnimationFrame(gxFrame);
  const d0=(now-GX.last)/1000||0.016,dt=Math.min(.05,d0);GX.last=now;
  GX.ema=GX.ema*.93+Math.min(.2,d0)*.07;
  if(GX.ema>.045&&GX.sc>.36&&now-GX.t0>2500){GX.sc=.36}
  gxSize();
  GX.p+=(GX.tp-GX.p)*(1-Math.exp(-dt*1.9));if(Math.abs(GX.tp-GX.p)<.001)GX.p=GX.tp;
  GX.w*=Math.exp(-dt*2.4);
  const g=GX.g;g.uniform2f(GX.u.R,GX.c.width,GX.c.height);g.uniform1f(GX.u.T,(now-GX.t0)/1000+20.);g.uniform1f(GX.u.P,GX.p);g.uniform1f(GX.u.W,GX.w);
  g.drawArrays(g.TRIANGLES,0,3)}
window.ZGX={GX,VB};function vbOff(){if(!VB.on)return;VB.on=0;cancelAnimationFrame(GX.raf);GX.raf=0;document.body.classList.remove('vbg');FC.style.visibility=''}
/* ---- Theme videos: one short cinematic loop per world, played behind the app. Files live in the repo folder assets/themes/ (cosmos, life, tech, art .mp4 + .jpg poster).
   To serve them from somewhere else (for example a public Supabase Storage bucket) change THEME_VIDEO_BASE only. If a file is missing the app falls back to its drawn background. ---- */
const THEME_VIDEO_BASE='assets/themes/';
const TV={w:'',s:-1,on:0,tp:null,ok:{},try:{},rate:0,cur:null,pf:{}};window.ZTV=TV;
function tvOff(){TV.on=0;TV.cur=null;TV.s=-1;document.body.classList.remove('tvon');for(const e of [TVE,TVE2]){e.classList.remove('tvf');try{e.pause()}catch(_){}}}
/* four stages per world: 0 = start / choose theme, 1 = Imagination, 2 = Photos, 3 = Animation; each has its own footage (assets/themes/<world>-<stage>.mp4) */
function tvSI(tp){return tp>=1?3:tp>=.68?2:tp>=.36?1:0}
function tvUrl(w,s){return THEME_VIDEO_BASE+w+'-'+s+'.mp4?v=3'}
function tvPre(w,s){if(s<0||s>3||LITE())return;const k=w+s;if(TV.pf[k])return;const e=document.createElement('video');e.preload='auto';e.muted=true;e.src=tvUrl(w,s);TV.pf[k]=e}
function tvPut(w,s){
 const key=w+'-'+s;if(TV.try[key])return;TV.try[key]=1;
 const nx=TV.cur===TVE?TVE2:TVE,old=TV.cur,urls=[tvUrl(w,s),THEME_VIDEO_BASE+w+'.mp4?v=2'];let k=0;
 const bad=()=>{TV.try[key]=0;nx.oncanplay=null;nx.onerror=null;if(!TV.on){TV.ok[w]=false;if(mode==='ws'&&cs&&cs.v)vbFor(cs.v)}};
 nx.onerror=()=>{k++;if(k<urls.length){nx.src=urls[k];nx.load()}else bad()};
 nx.oncanplay=()=>{nx.oncanplay=null;nx.onerror=null;TV.try[key]=0;
  if((getWorld()||'cosmos')!==w||mode!=='ws')return;
  const go=()=>{if((getWorld()||'cosmos')!==w||mode!=='ws')return;TV.w=w;TV.s=s;TV.cur=nx;TV.on=1;vbOff();FC.style.visibility='hidden';document.body.classList.add('tvon');
   nx.style.zIndex=1;if(old)old.style.zIndex=0;nx.classList.add('tvf');
   if(old&&old!==nx)setTimeout(()=>{if(TV.cur===nx){old.classList.remove('tvf');try{old.pause()}catch(_){}}},1200);
   tvPre(w,s+1)};
  nx.playbackRate=1;const pr=LITE()?null:nx.play();if(pr&&pr.then)pr.then(go).catch(()=>bad());else go()};
 if(LITE()){/* light mode: a still picture instead of moving footage, so slow phones and slow networks stay fast */
  const pics=[THEME_VIDEO_BASE+w+'-'+s+'.jpg',THEME_VIDEO_BASE+w+'.jpg'];let j=0;const im=new Image();
  im.onload=()=>{try{nx.pause()}catch(_){}nx.removeAttribute('src');nx.poster=im.src;nx.preload='none';if(nx.oncanplay)nx.oncanplay()};
  im.onerror=()=>{j++;if(j<pics.length)im.src=pics[j];else bad()};im.src=pics[0];return}
 nx.preload='auto';nx.muted=true;nx.loop=true;nx.src=urls[0];nx.load()}
function tvKick(){
 if(RM||mode!=='ws'){if(TV.on)tvOff();return false}
 const w=getWorld()||'cosmos';
 if(TV.ok[w]===false){if(TV.on)tvOff();return false}
 const tp=cs&&cs.v!==undefined?TPC[cs.v]:undefined,s=tvSI(tp===undefined?0:tp);
 if(TV.on&&TV.w===w&&TV.s===s)return true;
 if(TV.on&&TV.w===w)return true;   /* a stage change is handled by tvStage */
 tvPut(w,s);return !!TV.on}
/* a change of stage: light-speed streaks, a speed-up of the footage, and the NEXT stage's own footage fades in under the streaks, then everything settles */
function tvStage(v){const tp=TPC[v];if(tp===undefined)return;const dir=TV.tp===null?0:(tp>TV.tp?1:tp<TV.tp?-1:0);TV.tp=tp;const w=getWorld()||'cosmos',s=tvSI(tp);
 if(!TV.on||s===TV.s)return;if(dir)wpGo(dir);tvPut(w,s);
 const t0=performance.now();clearInterval(TV.rate);TV.rate=setInterval(()=>{const k=(performance.now()-t0)/1500,c=TV.cur;if(!c)return;if(k>=1){c.playbackRate=1;clearInterval(TV.rate);return}c.playbackRate=1+2.4*Math.pow(1-k,2)},60)}
function vbFor(v){
  if(tvKick()){tvStage(v);return}
  const cos=(getWorld()||'cosmos')==='cosmos'&&!RM;
  if(!cos||GX.ok<0){vbOff();return}
  if(!(v in TPC))return;
  if(!GX.ok){GX.ok=gxBuild()?1:-1;if(GX.ok<0){vbOff();return}}

  if(!VB.on){VB.on=1;GX.t0=performance.now();GX.last=performance.now();GX.p=GX.tp=tp;document.body.classList.add('vbg');FC.style.visibility='hidden';GX.raf=requestAnimationFrame(gxFrame);return}
  if(tp!==GX.tp){GX.w=1;wpGo(tp>GX.tp?1:-1);GX.tp=tp}}

function enterWs(){closeP();mode='ws';nav.hidden=true;document.body.classList.add('ws');WS.hidden=false;cancelAnimationFrame(raf);raf=0;cv.style.visibility='hidden';FC.style.display='block';fxSize();if(!FX.raf){FX.last=performance.now();FX.raf=requestAnimationFrame(fxFrame)}

/* The ONLY write path for edits and accepted suggestions. original_expression is not in FLDS, so it can never be sent. */
async function saveFields(id,patch,src){for(const k in patch)if(!FLDS.includes(k))throw new Error('Blocked field: '+k);if(!sb)return{ok:false,msg:'Not connected.'};
 try{const {data,error}=await sb.from('ideas').update({...patch,...(SQL12===true?{last_change_source:src||'you'}:{}),updated_at:new Date().toISOString()}).eq('id',id).select(COLS).single();
  if(error||!data)return{ok:false,msg:error?dbErr(error):'no row was updated.'};
  if(ideas)ideas=ideas.map(x=>x.id===data.id?data:x);lastRow=data;
  if(ed[id]){for(const k in patch)ed[id][k]=k==='title'?(data.title||''):data[k];recheck(id)}
  if(SQL12===true)ixEnsure(id,true);
  return{ok:true,row:data}}catch(err){return{ok:false,msg:dbErr(err)}}}

const CK={interpretation:['Interpretation','working_version'],ambiguity:['Clarifying question','questions'],alternative:['Alternative reading','working_version'],improvement:['Possible improvement','working_version'],related:['Related concept','notes'],question:['Worth investigating','questions'],limitation:['Possible limitation','notes'],next_step:['Possible next step','notes'],field:['Field','notes'],understanding:['AI understanding','notes'],hypothesis:['Hypothesis','notes'],reference:['To look up','notes']};
const CB={established:'Established knowledge',interpretation:'AI interpretation',assumption:'Assumption',hypothesis:'Hypothesis',unknown:'Unknown'};
const BE={established:'Widely verified knowledge.',interpretation:"The AI's reading of your idea. It may not be what you meant.",assumption:'Something the idea seems to take for granted.',hypothesis:'A guess that could be tested.',unknown:'Cannot be settled from what you wrote.'};

async function ciErr(e){const m=(e&&e.message)||'';if(m==='TIMEOUT')return 'The AI service took too long. Nothing was changed. Try again.';const st=e&&e.context&&e.context.status;let sv='';try{const j=await e.context.json();sv=j&&j.error||''}catch(_){}
 if(sv)return sv; /* the server's own readable message always wins */
 if(st===404||/not found.*function|function.*not found/i.test(m))return 'Creative Intelligence is not connected yet. No function named "'+CI_FN+'" was found. Check the function name in Supabase.';
 if(st===401)return 'Please log in again.';if(st===429)return 'Too many requests. Try again later.';
 if(!st&&/failed to send|fetch|network/i.test(m))return 'The request could not reach the function, or the browser blocked it (often CORS: check ALLOWED_ORIGINS, and that the function is named "'+CI_FN+'").';
 return 'The AI request failed'+(st?' (server status '+st+')':'')+'. Nothing was changed. The cause is in the Supabase function logs.'}

 flow:d=>`<ol class='vf'>${vq(d.steps).map(x=>`<li><div><b>${vt(x.title,80)}</b><span>${vt(x.text)}</span></div></li>`).join('')}</ol>`,
 timeline:d=>`<div class='vtl'>${vq(d.events).map(x=>`<div><i></i><small>${vt(x.when,40)}</small><b>${vt(x.title,80)}</b><span>${vt(x.text)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`,
 graph:d=>{const P=vq(d.points).map(x=>({l:String(x&&x.label||'').slice(0,30),v:Number(x&&x.value)})).filter(x=>x.l&&isFinite(x.v)&&x.v>=0);if(P.length<2)return "<p class='hint'>Not enough numbers to draw.</p>";
  const mx=Math.max(...P.map(x=>x.v))||1,H=P.length*38+8,u=String(d.unit||'').slice(0,20);
  return `${d.title?`<p><b>${vt(d.title,80)}</b></p>`:''}<svg class='vg' viewBox='0 0 320 ${H}' role='img' aria-label='Bar graph: ${esc(P.map(x=>x.l+' '+x.v+(u?' '+u:'')).join(', '))}'>${P.map((x,i)=>{const w=Math.max(2,Math.round(x.v/mx*130)),y=i*38+4;return `<text x='0' y='${y+14}' class='vgl'>${esc(x.l)}</text><rect x='110' y='${y}' width='${w}' height='20' rx='4' class='vgb'/><text x='${116+w}' y='${y+15}' class='vgv'>${esc(String(x.v)+(u?' '+u:''))}</text>`}).join('')}</svg><p class='hint'>Every number here comes from your own text. Zulora did not calculate or add any.</p>`},
 compare:d=>{const O=vq(d.options).map(x=>String(x).slice(0,40));return `<div class='vtb'><table><thead><tr><th></th>${O.map(o=>`<th>${esc(o)}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${vq(d.rows).map(r=>`<tr><th>${vt(r&&r.label,40)}</th>${vq(r&&r.cells).slice(0,O.length).map(c=>`<td>${vt(c,140)}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`},
 risks:d=>`<div class='vrk'>${vq(d.items).map(x=>`<div><b>${vt(x.risk,180)}</b><span><em>Try this:</em> ${vt(x.test,220)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`,
 journey:d=>`${d.who?`<p class='hint'>Following: ${vt(d.who,70)}</p>`:''}<ol class='vf'>${vq(d.stages).map(x=>`<li><div><b>${vt(x.title,60)}</b><span>${vt(x.text)}</span></div></li>`).join('')}</ol>`,
 people:d=>`<div class='vpp'>${vq(d.groups).map(x=>`<div><b>${vt(x.who,60)}</b><span><em>Could gain:</em> ${vt(x.gain,220)}</span><span><em>May worry:</em> ${vt(x.concern,220)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`,
 mood:d=>`<p>${vt(d.mood,220)}</p><div class='vsw'>${vq(d.colors).filter(c=>c&&/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(c.hex)).map(c=>`<div><i style='background:${c.hex}'></i><b>${esc(c.hex)}</b><span>${vt(c.meaning,70)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div><div class='vch'>${vq(d.symbols).map(x=>`<span>${vt(x,60)}</span>`).join('')}${vq(d.words).map(x=>`<span class='vwd'>${vt(x,40)}</span>`).join('')}</div>`,
 pitch:d=>`<div class='vpc'>${[['Problem',d.problem],['The idea',d.idea],['Why it matters',d.why],['What comes next',d.next]].map(x=>`<div><h4>${x[0]}</h4><p>${vt(x[1],240)}</p></div>`).join('')}</div>`};
function vwHTML(id){const V=vwGet(id),keys=Object.keys(VN).filter(k=>V.data[k]);
 const head="<h2>More ways to see your idea</h2><p>Not every idea needs the same picture. Zulora reads yours and offers the views that fit it, such as a flow, a timeline, a graph, a comparison or a mood board. They are drawn here, so they are exact and free. Uses 1 of your 20 AI uses per hour.</p>";
 const err=V.err?`<p class='ms er' role='alert'>${esc(V.err)}</p>`:'';
 if(V.state==='busy')return `<section class='zs'>${head}<p class='ms wn' role='status'>${V.busy?'Preparing the '+esc(VN[V.busy][0])+' view':'Choosing the views that fit your idea'}&hellip; about half a minute.</p></section>`;
 if(!keys.length)return `<section class='zs'>${head}${err}<button class='wg' data-w='vwgen'>Show me the views that fit my idea</button></section>`;
 const more=Object.keys(VN).filter(k=>!V.data[k]);
 return `<section class='zs'>${head}${err}${keys.map(k=>`<section class='vc vw'><h3>${esc(VN[k][0])}</h3><p class='hint'>${esc(VN[k][1])}</p>${vwBody[k](V.data[k])}<p class='cn'>AI working: one possible reading of your idea, not a fact. Check it yourself.</p><button class='lk2' data-w='vwdel:${k}'>Hide this view</button></section>`).join('')}${V.note?`<p class='ms wn'>${esc(V.note)}</p>`:''}${more.length?`<h3>Add another view</h3><div class='vch vadd'>${more.map(k=>`<button class='lk2' data-w='vwadd:${k}'>${esc(VN[k][0])}</button>`).join('')}</div>`:''}</section>`}
/* ---- Imagination: what Zulora understands about the idea (field-aware), saved with the idea ---- */
const IGL=(S,k)=>S.understand.filter(x=>x.kind===k);
const IGI=x=>`<div class='igi'><p class='rt'>${esc(x.text)}</p>${x.why?`<small>${esc(x.why)}</small>`:''}<span class='ci-b'>${CB[x.basis]}</span></div>`;
const SEARCH=q=>{const e=encodeURIComponent(q);return `<span class='lnk'><a href='https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?search=${e}' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Search Wikipedia</a><a href='https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=${e}' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Search Google Scholar</a></span>`};
/* ---- Knowledge Hub: the user accepts, modifies or rejects each AI item; accepted ones are stored in their Hub ---- */
let KN_OFF=false;
const knKey=x=>x.kind+'|'+x.text;
async function knLoad(id){const S=CIS(id);if(!sb)return;
 try{const {data,error}=await sb.from('knowledge_items').select('kind,ai_text,user_text,decision').eq('idea_id',id);
  if(error)KN_OFF=true;else{KN_OFF=false;S.kn={};for(const r of data||[])S.kn[r.kind+'|'+r.ai_text]={decision:r.decision,user:r.user_text||''}}}
 catch(_){KN_OFF=true}
 redraw(id)}
async function knAct(act,iid){const id=cs.id,S=id&&CI[id];if(!S)return;const x=S.understand.find(z=>z.id===iid);if(!x)return;S.kn=S.kn||{};const k=knKey(x);
 if(act==='mod'){S.kmod=iid;S.knNote='';draw2();return}
 if(act==='cancel'){S.kmod='';draw2();return}
 if(act==='undo'){delete S.kn[k];S.kmod='';draw2();if(sb&&!KN_OFF){try{await sb.from('knowledge_items').delete().eq('idea_id',id).eq('kind',x.kind).eq('ai_text',x.text)}catch(_){}}return}
 const dec=act==='rej'?'rejected':'accepted';let user='';
 if(act==='save'){const t=WS.querySelector('#km_'+iid);user=t?t.value.trim().slice(0,1200):'';if(!user){S.knNote='Write your own version first, or choose Accept to keep the AI wording.';draw2();return}if(user===x.text)user=''}
 S.kmod='';S.knNote='';S.kn[k]={decision:dec,user};draw2();
 if(sb&&!KN_OFF){try{const {error}=await sb.from('knowledge_items').upsert({idea_id:id,kind:x.kind,perspective:(x.lens||'').slice(0,60),ai_text:x.text,user_text:user,why:x.why||'',basis:x.basis,decision:dec,updated_at:new Date().toISOString()},{onConflict:'idea_id,kind,ai_text'});
  if(error){KN_OFF=true;S.knNote='Not saved to your Knowledge Hub: the storage step (SQL 05) has not been run. Your choice is kept only until you reload.';draw2()}}catch(_){KN_OFF=true;S.knNote='Could not save to your Knowledge Hub. Your choice is kept only until you reload.';draw2()}}}
const LENSES=['Science','Art','Design','Technology','Philosophy','Society','Business','Story'];
const lk=t=>(t||'').trim().toLowerCase();
async function perspAdd(id,name){const S=CIS(id);name=(name||'').replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9 &,'\-]/g,'').trim().slice(0,40);if(!name||S.busy||S.pbusy||!sb)return;
 S.pbusy=name;S.perr='';redraw(id);
 try{if(!sb.functions)throw new Error('LIBRARY');
  const r=await Promise.race([sb.functions.invoke(CI_FN,{body:{idea_id:id,mode:'understand',perspective:name}}),new Promise((_,j)=>setTimeout(()=>j(new Error('TIMEOUT')),35000))]);
  if(r.error)throw r.error;const ok=ciClean(r.data&&r.data.items).filter(x=>x.kind!=='reference');if(!ok.length)throw new Error('bad');
  const seen=new Set(S.understand.map(knKey));for(const x of ok){if(x.kind!=='field'&&!x.lens)x.lens=name;if(!seen.has(knKey(x)))S.understand.push(x)}
  if(!S.understand.some(x=>x.kind==='field'&&lk(x.text)===lk(name)))S.understand.push({id:'c'+(++cn),kind:'field',basis:'interpretation',text:name,why:'',lens:''});
  insSave(id)}
 catch(e){S.perr=await ciErr(e)}
 S.pbusy='';redraw(id)}
/* real matches for each "reference" topic: Wikipedia and OpenAlex searches made from the user's phone. Nothing is invented: only pages the search services return are shown. */
const REFC={};
function refKick(id,list){for(const x of list){const q=x.text.trim();if(!q||REFC[q])continue;REFC[q]={st:'load',w:[],a:[]};refFetch(q,id)}}
const unHtml=t=>{const e=document.createElement('textarea');e.innerHTML=String(t||'').replace(/<[^>]*>/g,'');return e.value};
async function refFetch(q,id){const R=REFC[q],e=encodeURIComponent(q);
 const get=async u=>{const c=new AbortController(),t=setTimeout(()=>c.abort(),9000);try{const r=await fetch(u,{signal:c.signal});if(!r.ok)throw new Error('http');return await r.json()}finally{clearTimeout(t)}};
 const [w,a]=await Promise.allSettled([get('https://en.wikipedia.org/w/api.php?action=query&list=search&srsearch='+e+'&srlimit=3&format=json&origin=*&srprop=snippet'),get('https://api.openalex.org/works?search='+e+'&per-page=3&select=id,display_name,publication_year,doi,primary_location')]);
 if(w.status==='fulfilled'){for(const h of ((w.value&&w.value.query&&w.value.query.search)||[]))if(h&&typeof h.title==='string'&&Number.isInteger(h.pageid))R.w.push({t:h.title.slice(0,120),u:'https://en.wikipedia.org/?curid='+h.pageid,s:unHtml(h.snippet).slice(0,170)})}
 if(a.status==='fulfilled'){for(const h of ((a.value&&a.value.results)||[])){const u=typeof h.doi==='string'&&/^https:\/\/doi\.org\/10\./.test(h.doi)?h.doi:(typeof h.id==='string'&&/^https:\/\/openalex\.org\/W\d+$/.test(h.id)?h.id:'');
  if(u&&typeof h.display_name==='string')R.a.push({t:h.display_name.slice(0,160),u,y:Number.isInteger(h.publication_year)?h.publication_year:0,j:(h.primary_location&&h.primary_location.source&&typeof h.primary_location.source.display_name==='string')?h.primary_location.source.display_name.slice(0,80):''})}}
 R.st=(w.status==='fulfilled'||a.status==='fulfilled')?'ok':'err';redraw(id)}
const RLINK=(u,t)=>`<a href='${esc(u)}' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>${esc(t)}</a>`;
function REFS(x){const R=REFC[x.text.trim()]||{st:'load',w:[],a:[]};let m='';
 if(R.st==='load')m="<small>Looking for real matches&hellip;</small>";
 else if(R.st==='err')m="<small>The search services could not be reached from your phone. Use the links below.</small>";
 else if(!R.w.length&&!R.a.length)m="<small>No matching page was found for these words. Try the search links below.</small>";
 else m=`<div class='rl'>${R.w.length?`<b>Encyclopedia pages</b>${R.w.map(h=>RLINK(h.u,h.t)+(h.s?`<small>${esc(h.s)}</small>`:'')).join('')}`:''}${R.a.length?`<b>Research papers</b>${R.a.map(h=>RLINK(h.u,h.t)+`<small>${[h.y||'',esc(h.j)].filter(Boolean).join(' &middot; ')}</small>`).join('')}`:''}</div>`;
 return m}
function IGS(i,S,v){
 if(S.busy==='understand')return "<p class='ms wn' role='status'>Zulora is reading your idea&hellip; this can take up to half a minute.</p>";
 if(S.err.understand)return `<p class='ms er' role='alert'>${esc(S.err.understand)}</p><button class='wg' data-w='rrun:understand'>Try again</button>`;
 if(S.insLoading&&!S.ran.understand)return "<p class='ms wn' role='status'>Opening what Zulora understood before&hellip;</p>";
 if(!S.ran.understand)return `<p>Zulora reads your idea from the perspectives that fit it: scientific, artistic, technical, human or any other. You decide what to keep.</p><p class='hint'>Sends this idea's text to an external AI service (a free NVIDIA-hosted model). Uses 1 of your 20 AI uses per hour. Your idea is not changed.</p><button class='wg' data-w='rrun:understand'>Understand my idea</button>`;
 S.kn=S.kn||{};
 const f=IGL(S,'field'),r=IGL(S,'reference'),mine=(v.questions||'').trim(),names=[],seen=new Set();
 for(const x of f){const k=lk(x.text);if(k&&!seen.has(k)){seen.add(k);names.push(x.text)}}
 for(const x of S.understand){if(x.kind==='field'||x.kind==='reference')continue;const k=lk(x.lens)||'overview';if(!seen.has(k)){seen.add(k);names.push(x.lens||'Overview')}}
 const DK=x=>{const d=S.kn[knKey(x)];
  if(S.kmod===x.id)return `<div class='dk'><textarea id='km_${x.id}' rows='4' maxlength='1200' aria-label='Your version'>${esc((d&&d.user)||x.text)}</textarea><div class='db'><button class='dkb ac' data-w='kn:save' data-i='${x.id}'>Save my version</button><button class='dkb' data-w='kn:cancel' data-i='${x.id}'>Cancel</button></div></div>`;
  if(d&&d.decision==='accepted')return `<div class='dk'><span class='dt ok'>&#10003; ${d.user?'Accepted with your own wording':'Accepted'} &middot; in your Knowledge Hub</span><div class='db'><button class='dkb' data-w='kn:mod' data-i='${x.id}'>Modify</button><button class='dkb' data-w='kn:undo' data-i='${x.id}'>Undo</button></div></div>`;
  if(d&&d.decision==='rejected')return `<div class='dk'><span class='dt'>Rejected</span><div class='db'><button class='dkb' data-w='kn:undo' data-i='${x.id}'>Undo</button></div></div>`;
  return `<div class='dk'><div class='db'><button class='dkb ac' data-w='kn:acc' data-i='${x.id}'>Accept</button><button class='dkb' data-w='kn:mod' data-i='${x.id}'>Modify</button><button class='dkb rj' data-w='kn:rej' data-i='${x.id}'>Reject</button></div></div>`};
 const ZI=(x,mk,pre)=>{const d=S.kn[knKey(x)],rj=d&&d.decision==='rejected',shown=d&&d.user?d.user:x.text;
  return `<div class='zi${rj?' rjd':''}'><span class='zm'>${mk}</span><div><p>${esc(shown)}</p>${d&&d.user?`<small>Zulora wrote: ${esc(x.text)}</small>`:''}${x.why&&!rj?`<small>${pre||''}${esc(x.why)}</small>`:''}<span class='zb'>${d&&d.user?'Your wording':CB[x.basis]}</span>${DK(x)}</div></div>`};
 const inLens=(x,n)=>(lk(x.lens)||'overview')===lk(n);
 const cards=names.map((n,k)=>{const u=S.understand.filter(x=>x.kind==='understanding'&&inLens(x,n)),q=S.understand.filter(x=>x.kind==='question'&&inLens(x,n)),h=S.understand.filter(x=>x.kind==='hypothesis'&&inLens(x,n)),fw=f.find(x=>lk(x.text)===lk(n));
  return `<section class='zs'><h2><span>${k+1}</span>${esc(n)}</h2>${fw&&fw.why?`<p class='fw'>${esc(fw.why)}</p>`:''}${u.length?`<h3 class='zh'>How Zulora reads it</h3>${u.map((x,j)=>ZI(x,j+1)).join('')}`:''}${q.length?`<h3 class='zh'>Questions worth asking</h3>${q.map(x=>ZI(x,'?')).join('')}`:''}${h.length?`<h3 class='zh'>Hypothesis</h3>${h.map((x,j)=>ZI(x,'H'+(j+1),'How it could be tested: ')).join('')}`:''}${!u.length&&!q.length&&!h.length?"<p class='hint'>Nothing was returned for this perspective.</p>":''}</section>`}).join('');
 const acc=Object.values(S.kn).filter(d=>d.decision==='accepted').length;
 const addable=LENSES.filter(l=>!seen.has(lk(l)));
 refKick(i.id,r);
 return `${KN_OFF?"<p class='ms wn' role='status'>Your Accept, Modify and Reject choices are kept only until you reload. The Knowledge Hub storage step (SQL 05) has not been run.</p>":''}${S.knNote?`<p class='ms wn' role='status'>${esc(S.knNote)}</p>`:''}
 <p class='hint'>Nothing below becomes your knowledge until you accept it. Accept it as written, modify it into your own words, or reject it. Accepted items go to your Knowledge Hub in Profile.${acc?` You have accepted ${acc} for this idea.`:''}</p>
 ${cards}
 ${mine?`<section class='zs'><h2><span>&bull;</span>Your own questions</h2><div class='zi mine'><span class='zm'>&bull;</span><div><p style='white-space:pre-wrap'>${esc(mine)}</p><span class='zb'>Your own questions</span></div></div></section>`:''}
 <section class='zs'><h2><span>+</span>Another perspective</h2><p class='hint' style='margin:0 0 6px!important'>Zulora chose the perspectives above from your idea. Ask for another point of view. Each one uses 1 of your 20 AI uses per hour.</p>${S.pbusy?`<p class='ms wn' role='status'>Reading your idea as ${esc(S.pbusy)}&hellip;</p>`:`<div class='tgs'>${addable.map(l=>`<button class='tg tgb' data-w='persp:${l}'>${l}</button>`).join('')}</div><div class='lf'><input id='lensin' maxlength='40' placeholder='Your own: Music, Law, Faith&hellip;' aria-label='Another perspective'><button class='wg' data-w='perspgo' style='margin:0'>Add</button></div>`}${S.perr?`<p class='ms er' role='alert'>${esc(S.perr)}</p>`:''}</section>
 <section class='zs'><h2><span>&#8599;</span>Reference materials</h2><p class='hint' style='margin:0 0 4px!important'>Zulora asks the AI only for topics to look up. The pages below were found by searching Wikipedia and OpenAlex for those words, so they exist, but Zulora has not checked that each one fits your idea: read the title before you rely on it. References are not saved to your Knowledge Hub.</p>${r.length?r.map(x=>`<div class='zi'><span class='zm'>&#8599;</span><div><p>${esc(x.text)}</p>${x.why?`<small>${esc(x.why)}</small>`:''}${REFS(x)}${SEARCH(x.text)}</div></div>`).join(''):"<p class='hint'>Nothing to look up was suggested.</p>"}</section>
 ${S.insNote?`<p class='ms wn' role='status'>${esc(S.insNote)}</p>`:`<p class='zf'>${S.insAt?'Saved with this idea '+D(S.insAt)+'. ':''}AI-written and not verified. It never replaces what you wrote.</p>`}<button class='lk2' data-w='rrun:understand'>Ask Zulora again</button>`}
const tx=(k,l,v,r)=>`<label for='f_${k}'>${l}</label><textarea id='f_${k}'${r?` rows='${r}'`:''}>${esc(v[k])}</textarea>`;
const SAVEB="<button class='wg' data-w='upd'>Save changes</button>";
const NOTB=t=>`<p class='nb'><b>Not built yet.</b> ${t}</p>`;
/* ---- TEMPORARY: image engine test (NVIDIA FLUX.2 klein 4B via hyper-api). Not part of the journey. ---- */
const VE_FN=CI_FN; /* text and images are served by the SAME function (hyper-api); requests are told apart by {action:'image'} */
const VT={prompt:'a simple coffee shop interior',busy:false,err:'',img:''};
async function veErr(e){const m=(e&&e.message)||'';if(m==='TIMEOUT')return 'The image service took too long. Nothing was saved. Try again.';const st=e&&e.context&&e.context.status;let sv='';try{const j=await e.context.json();sv=j&&j.error||''}catch(_){}
 if(sv)return sv;if(st===404)return 'The image engine is not connected yet. No function named "'+VE_FN+'" was found. Check the name in Supabase.';if(st===401)return 'Please log in again.';
 if(!st&&/failed to send|fetch|network/i.test(m))return 'The request could not reach the function, or the browser blocked it (often CORS: check ALLOWED_ORIGINS, and that the function is named "'+VE_FN+'").';
 return 'The image request failed'+(st?' (server status '+st+')':'')+'. The cause is in the Supabase function logs.'}
async function vGen(){if(VT.busy||!sb)return;const pr=VT.prompt.trim();if(pr.length<3){VT.err='Write a prompt of at least 3 characters.';draw2();return}
 VT.busy=true;VT.err='';VT.img='';draw2();
 try{if(!sb.functions)throw new Error('LIBRARY');
  const r=await Promise.race([sb.functions.invoke(VE_FN,{body:{action:'image',prompt:pr}}),new Promise((_,j)=>setTimeout(()=>j(new Error('TIMEOUT')),100000))]);
  if(r.error)throw r.error;const im=r.data&&r.data.image;
  if(typeof im!=='string'||!/^data:image\/(png|jpeg|webp);base64,[A-Za-z0-9+\/]+={0,2}$/.test(im))throw new Error('bad');
  VT.img=im}
 catch(e){VT.err=e&&e.message==='bad'?'The image engine replied with something that is not a picture.':await veErr(e)}
 VT.busy=false;if(mode==='ws'&&cs.v==='vtest')draw2()}
/* ---- Stage III visual exploration: THREE pictures per exploration (concept / technical / developed), saved per idea ----
   1) hyper-api {action:'visual_plan',idea_id}  -> Nemotron writes three different picture descriptions from the idea
   2) hyper-api {action:'image',prompt,idea_id,kind,set_id} x3, one at a time -> FLUX.2 klein 4B draws each; the function saves it to private Storage
   Saved pictures are read back with the signed-in user's own session (Storage + table policies), so they survive refresh and re-login. */
let animStyle=null;
const AF=[['2d','2D','Flat, graphic animation. Elements flow and pulse in place, with no camera depth.'],['3d','3D','The camera moves around your picture and shows its depth and volume.'],['simulation','Simulation','Your idea shown working: parts moving, energy and flow, with a steady camera.'],['pitch','Pitch','A cinematic reveal with transitions and sweeping light, made to present your idea.']];
const ANIMTOP=(v,vis)=>`<h1>Animation</h1><p>Step 1. Choose the format.
