 <div class='cau'><b>Before you publish.</b> Anyone who is logged in to Zulora can read and copy what you publish, and Zulora cannot stop them or prove you had the idea first. Do not publish anything private or secret, or anything that belongs to someone else.</div>
 <label class='ck'><input type='checkbox' id='mvok'> I understand, and I want ${ex?'to update this public copy':'to make this idea public'}. People can build on a private copy of it, and my name stays attached as the source.</label><p class='ms' id='mvm' role='status'></p><button class='wg' data-w='mvdo'>${ex?'Update public copy':'Make public'}</button>${ex?`<button class='lk2' data-w='mvun:${esc(ex.id)}'>Delete from feed</button><p class='hint'>Your original private idea stays with you. Zulora keeps a private record of what was published and who contributed.</p>${AGR()}`:''}`};
/* ---- Analytics for a published idea (SQL 10): KPIs with change vs the previous period, trend with a ghost of the previous period, funnel, weekday pattern, calendar heatmap, weekly mix, comparison across ideas, CSV, and an AI explanation matched to the reader's age group ---- */
const GS=[['views','Views','#3987e5','circle',''],['searches','Searches','#d95926','square','6 4'],['resonates','Resonates','#199e70','triangle','2 4'],['comments','Comments','#c98500','diamond','9 3 2 3'],['suggestions','Suggestions','#d55181','cross','']];
const SK=GS.map(s=>s[0]),GCOL={all:'#8ec0ff'};GS.forEach(s=>GCOL[s[0]]=s[2]);
const GR={wid:null,days:30,metric:'all',smooth:false,all:{},ins:{},busy:false,err:'',sel:null,hm:null,tbl:false,lerr:''};
const ageBand=()=>{const d=PF.row&&PF.row.date_of_birth;if(!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(d||''))return 'general';const b=new Date(d+'T00:00:00Z'),n=new Date();let a=n.getUTCFullYear()-b.getUTCFullYear();if(n.getUTCMonth()<b.getUTCMonth()||(n.getUTCMonth()===b.getUTCMonth()&&n.getUTCDate()<b.getUTCDate()))a--;return a<13?'child':a<18?'teen':'general'};
const gtot=r=>SK.reduce((a,k)=>a+r[k],0),gsum=(rows,k)=>rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+(k==='all'?gtot(r):r[k]),0);
const gmet=(r,k)=>k==='all'?gtot(r):r[k];
const gslice=(wid,d)=>{const A=GR.all[wid]||[];return{cur:A.slice(-d),prev:A.slice(-2*d,-d)}};
const gdelta=(c,p)=>c===p?{t:'▬ no change',d:0}:p===0?{t:'▲ new',d:1}:{t:(c>p?'▲ +':'▼ −')+Math.abs(Math.round((c-p)/p*100))+'%',d:c>p?1:-1};
const grate=rows=>{const v=gsum(rows,'views');return v?Math.round((gsum(rows,'resonates')+gsum(rows,'comments')+gsum(rows,'suggestions'))/v*100):null};
const fmtD=s=>{const d=new Date(s+'T00:00:00Z');return isNaN(d)?s:d.toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{day:'numeric',month:'short',timeZone:'UTC'})};
const wdi=s=>(new Date(s+'T00:00:00Z').getUTCDay()+6)%7;
async function grLoadOne(id){if(!sb)return;
 try{const {data,error}=await sb.rpc('work_activity',{p_work:id,p_days:180});if(error)throw error;GR.all[id]=(data||[]).map(r=>({day:String(r.day).slice(0,10),views:+r.views||0,searches:+r.searches||0,resonates:+r.resonates||0,comments:+r.comments||0,suggestions:+r.suggestions||0}));GR.lerr=''}
 catch(e){GR.lerr=/42883|does not exist|schema cache|PGRST20|42P01/i.test(String(e&&(e.message||e.code)))?'The activity step (SQL 10) has not been run yet, so there is nothing to draw.':'Could not load the activity: '+dbErr(e);GR.all[id]=[]}}
async function grEnsure(){const M=MV.mine||[];if(!M.length){GR.wid=null;return}if(!GR.wid||!M.some(x=>x.id===GR.wid))GR.wid=M[0].id;
 const need=M.slice(0,6).filter(x=>!GR.all[x.id]);await Promise.all(need.map(x=>grLoadOne(x.id)));if(need.length&&mode==='ws'&&cs.v==='hub')draw2()}
async function grExplain(){if(GR.busy||!GR.wid)return;GR.busy=true;GR.err='';draw2();const k=GR.wid+':'+GR.days;
 try{if(!PF.loaded)await profFetch();const r=await vsCall({action:'insight',work_id:GR.wid,days:GR.days,band:ageBand()},80000),d=r.data;if(!d||typeof d.summary!=='string')throw new Error('bad');GR.ins[k]=d}
 catch(e){GR.err=e&&e.message==='bad'?'The AI reply could not be read. Try again.':await ciErr(e)}
 GR.busy=false;draw2()}
function grCsv(){const A=GR.all[GR.wid]||[],w=(MV.mine||[]).find(x=>x.id===GR.wid),t='day,views,searches,resonates,comments,suggestions\n'+A.map(r=>[r.day,r.views,r.searches,r.resonates,r.comments,r.suggestions].join(',')).join('\n');
 try{const u=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([t],{type:'text/csv'})),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=u;a.download='zulora-activity-'+(String((w&&w.title)||'idea').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-').slice(0,30)||'idea')+'.csv';document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();setTimeout(()=>{URL.revokeObjectURL(u);a.remove()},500);MV.note=''}catch(_){}}
function grShape(t,x,y,c){const r=4.5,f=`fill='${c}' stroke='#0a0f24' stroke-width='2'`;
 return t==='square'?`<rect x='${x-r}' y='${y-r}' width='${2*r}' height='${2*r}' ${f}/>`:t==='triangle'?`<path d='M${x} ${y-r-1}L${x+r+1} ${y+r}L${x-r-1} ${y+r}Z' ${f}/>`:t==='diamond'?`<path d='M${x} ${y-r-1}L${x+r+1} ${y}L${x} ${y+r+1}L${x-r-1} ${y}Z' ${f}/>`:t==='cross'?`<path d='M${x-r} ${y-r}L${x+r} ${y+r}M${x+r} ${y-r}L${x-r} ${y+r}' stroke='${c}' stroke-width='3' fill='none'/>`:`<circle cx='${x}' cy='${y}' r='${r}' ${f}/>`}
const gspark=(vals,c,w,h)=>{w=w||64;h=h||20;const n=vals.length,mx=Math.max(1,...vals);if(n<2)return '';return `<svg class='gsp' width='${w}' height='${h}' viewBox='0 0 ${w} ${h}' aria-hidden='true' preserveAspectRatio='none'><polyline fill='none' vector-effect='non-scaling-stroke' stroke='${c}' stroke-width='1.6' stroke-linejoin='round' points='${vals.map((v,i)=>(i*(w-2)/(n-1)+1).toFixed(1)+','+(h-2-(v/mx)*(h-4)).toFixed(1)).join(' ')}'/></svg>`};
function movAvg(v,k){return v.map((_,i)=>{const a=Math.max(0,i-k+1),s=v.slice(a,i+1);return s.reduce((x,y)=>x+y,0)/s.length})}
function grTrend(cur,prev,w){const n=cur.length;if(!n)return '';const W=340,H=200,L=32,R=332,T=26,B=164,dx=n>1?(R-L)/(n-1):0,X=i=>Math.round((L+i*dx)*10)/10,m=GR.metric,col=GCOL[m]||'#8ec0ff';
 let vc=cur.map(r=>gmet(r,m)),vp=prev.map(r=>gmet(r,m));if(GR.smooth){vc=movAvg(vc,7);vp=movAvg(vp,7)}
 let mx=Math.max(3,...vc,...vp);if(mx>12)mx=Math.ceil(mx/4)*4;const Y=v=>Math.round((B-(v/mx)*(B-T))*10)/10;
 const grid=[0,.5,1].map(f=>{const v=Math.round(mx*f*10)/10,y=Y(v);return `<line x1='${L}' x2='${R}' y1='${y}' y2='${y}' stroke='rgba(201,210,238,.16)'/><text x='${L-6}' y='${y+4}' text-anchor='end' class='gax'>${Number.isInteger(v)?v:v.toFixed(1)}</text>`}).join('');
 const xl=[0,Math.floor((n-1)/2),n-1].filter((v,i,a)=>a.indexOf(v)===i).map(i=>`<text x='${X(i)}' y='${B+18}' text-anchor='${i===0?'start':i===n-1?'end':'middle'}' class='gax'>${fmtD(cur[i].day)}</text>`).join('');
 const ev=[];if(w){const p=String(w.created_at||'').slice(0,10),u=String(w.updated_at||'').slice(0,10),ip=cur.findIndex(r=>r.day===p),iu=cur.findIndex(r=>r.day===u);if(ip>=0)ev.push([ip,'Published']);if(iu>=0&&iu!==ip)ev.push([iu,'Updated'])}
 const evs=ev.map(([i,t])=>`<line x1='${X(i)}' x2='${X(i)}' y1='${T-6}' y2='${B}' stroke='#f0efec' stroke-width='1.5' stroke-dasharray='3 3' opacity='.75'/><text x='${Math.min(Math.max(X(i),L+22),R-22)}' y='${T-10}' text-anchor='middle' class='gev'>${t}</text>`).join('');
 const pl=pts=>pts.map((v,i)=>X(i)+','+Y(v)).join(' '),area=`M${X(0)},${B} L${vc.map((v,i)=>X(i)+','+Y(v)).join(' L')} L${X(n-1)},${B}Z`;
 const si=GR.sel==null||GR.sel>=n?n-1:GR.sel,cross=`<line x1='${X(si)}' x2='${X(si)}' y1='${T}' y2='${B}' stroke='#d6b26a' stroke-width='1.5'/>`;
 const hit=cur.map((r,i)=>`<rect x='${Math.max(0,X(i)-Math.max(dx/2,5))}' y='${T}' width='${Math.max(dx,10)}' height='${B-T}' fill='transparent' class='grh' data-w='grday:${i}'><title>${fmtD(r.day)}</title></rect>`).join('');
 const si0=GR.sel==null||GR.sel>=n?n-1:GR.sel,dots=vc.map((v,i)=>(n<=10&&gmet(cur[i],m)>0)||i===si0?grShape('circle',X(i),Y(v),col):'').join('');
 return `<svg class='grs' viewBox='0 0 ${W} ${H}' role='img' aria-label='${esc((m==='all'?'All activity':m)+' per day, last '+n+' days compared with the '+prev.length+' days before. Total '+gsum(cur,m)+' now and '+gsum(prev,m)+' before.')}'><defs><linearGradient id='gfa' x1='0' y1='0' x2='0' y2='1'><stop offset='0' stop-color='${col}' stop-opacity='.35'/><stop offset='1' stop-color='${col}' stop-opacity='0'/></linearGradient></defs>${grid}${evs}${xl}<path d='${area}' fill='url(#gfa)'/>${prev.length>1?`<polyline fill='none' stroke='#a7a79c' stroke-width='1.6' stroke-dasharray='4 4' points='${pl(vp.slice(0,n))}'/>`:''}<polyline fill='none' stroke='${col}' stroke-width='2.4' stroke-linejoin='round' stroke-linecap='round' points='${pl(vc)}'/>${dots}${cross}${hit}</svg>`}
function grFunnel(cur){const st=[['Searches','searches'],['Views','views'],['Resonates','resonates'],['Comments','comments'],['Suggestions','suggestions']],v=st.map(s=>gsum(cur,s[1])),mx=Math.max(1,...v);
 return `<div class='gfn'>${st.map((s,i)=>{const w=Math.max(v[i]?3:0,Math.round(v[i]/mx*100)),pr=i&&v[i-1]>0?Math.round(v[i]/v[i-1]*100):null;return `<div class='gfr'><span class='gfl'>${s[0]}</span><span class='gfb'><i style='width:${w}%;background:${GCOL[s[1]]}'></i></span><b>${v[i]}</b><small>${pr==null?'':pr+' per 100 '+st[i-1][0].toLowerCase()}</small></div>`}).join('')}</div>`}
function grWeekday(cur){const t=[0,0,0,0,0,0,0];cur.forEach(r=>{t[wdi(r.day)]+=gtot(r)});const mx=Math.max(1,...t),best=t.indexOf(Math.max(...t)),N=['Mon','Tue','Wed','Thu','Fri','Sat','Sun'];
 return `<svg class='grs' viewBox='0 0 340 150' role='img' aria-label='Activity by day of the week. ${t.every(x=>!x)?'No activity.':'Busiest day: '+N[best]+'.'}'>${t.map((v,i)=>{const h=Math.round(v/mx*64),x=14+i*46;return `<rect x='${x}' y='${116-h}' width='34' height='${h}' rx='4' fill='${i===best&&v>0?'#3987e5':'#2b4a7a'}'/><text x='${x+17}' y='134' text-anchor='middle' class='gax'>${N[i]}</text><text x='${x+17}' y='${110-h}' text-anchor='middle' class='gvl'>${v||''}</text>${i===best&&v>0?`<text x='${x+17}' y='${96-h}' text-anchor='middle' class='gev'>Best</text>`:''}`}).join('')}</svg>`}
function grHeat(A){const rows=A.slice(-84);if(!rows.length)return '';const off=wdi(rows[0].day),cells=[];rows.forEach((r,i)=>cells.push({r,i,c:Math.floor((i+off)/7),y:(i+off)%7}));const cols=Math.floor((rows.length-1+off)/7)+1,mx=Math.max(1,...rows.map(gtot)),lv=v=>v<=0?0:v<=mx*.25?1:v<=mx*.5?2:v<=mx*.75?3:4,RAMP=['rgba(201,210,238,.08)','#1f3f73','#2d62ad','#3987e5','#9cc7ff'],cw=Math.min(22,Math.floor(300/cols)-3),W=cols*(cw+3)+22;
 const sel=GR.hm==null||GR.hm>=rows.length?rows.length-1:GR.hm,N=['M','T','W','T','F','S','S'];
 return `<svg class='grs' viewBox='0 0 ${W} ${7*(cw+3)+4}' role='img' aria-label='Calendar of activity for the last ${rows.length} days. Darker squares mean more activity.'>${[0,2,4].map(y=>`<text x='0' y='${y*(cw+3)+cw-3}' class='gax'>${N[y]}</text>`).join('')}${cells.map(o=>`<rect x='${22+o.c*(cw+3)}' y='${o.y*(cw+3)}' width='${cw}' height='${cw}' rx='3' fill='${RAMP[lv(gtot(o.r))]}' ${o.i===sel?"stroke='#d6b26a' stroke-width='2'":''} class='grh' data-w='grhm:${o.i}'><title>${fmtD(o.r.day)}: ${gtot(o.r)}</title></rect>`).join('')}</svg><p class='grd' role='status'><b>${fmtD(rows[sel].day)}</b>: ${GS.map(s=>s[1]+' '+rows[sel][s[0]]).join(' &middot; ')}</p><div class='ghl' aria-hidden='true'><small>Less</small>${RAMP.map(c=>`<i style='background:${c}'></i>`).join('')}<small>More</small></div>`}
function grMix(A){const rows=A.slice(-84),wk=[];for(let e=rows.length;e>0;e-=7)wk.unshift(rows.slice(Math.max(0,e-7),e));if(!wk.length)return '';
 const tot=wk.map(w=>SK.map(k=>gsum(w,k))),mx=Math.max(3,...tot.map(t=>t.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0))),bw=Math.min(22,Math.floor(300/wk.length)-4),H=150,B=130;
 return `<svg class='grs' viewBox='0 0 340 ${H+14}' role='img' aria-label='Weekly activity split by type, last ${wk.length} weeks.'>${wk.map((w,i)=>{let y=B;const x=28+i*(bw+4);const seg=SK.map((k,si)=>{const v=tot[i][si],h=Math.round(v/mx*(B-14));if(!v)return '';y-=h;return `<rect x='${x}' y='${y}' width='${bw}' height='${Math.max(h-2,1)}' rx='2' fill='${GCOL[k]}'><title>${esc(GS[si][1])}: ${v}</title></rect>`}).join('');return seg+(i%3===0||i===wk.length-1?`<text x='${x+bw/2}' y='${B+14}' text-anchor='middle' class='gax'>${fmtD(w[0].day)}</text>`:'')}).join('')}<line x1='24' x2='336' y1='${B}' y2='${B}' stroke='rgba(201,210,238,.3)'/><text x='22' y='20' text-anchor='end' class='gax'>${mx}</text></svg>`}
function grHTML(){const M=MV.mine||[];if(!M.length)return "<p class='hint'>Publish an idea first. Once people find it, view it, resonate or comment, its analytics will appear here and keep changing.</p>";
 if(!GR.wid||!M.some(x=>x.id===GR.wid))GR.wid=M[0].id;const w=M.find(x=>x.id===GR.wid),A=GR.all[GR.wid];
 const picker=M.length>1?`<div class='wk'>Idea</div><div class='vch vadd'>${M.map(x=>`<button class='lk2' data-w='grwork:${esc(x.id)}' aria-pressed='${x.id===GR.wid}'>${esc((x.title||'Untitled').slice(0,28))}</button>`).join('')}</div>`:`<p class='hint'><b>${esc(w.title||'Untitled')}</b></p>`;
 const range=`<div class='wk'>Period</div><div class='vch vadd'>${[7,30,90].map(d=>`<button class='lk2' data-w='grdays:${d}' aria-pressed='${GR.days===d}'>Last ${d} days</button>`).join('')}</div><p class='hint'>Each number below is compared with the ${GR.days} days before it.</p>`;
 if(GR.lerr)return picker+range+`<p class='ms er' role='alert'>${esc(GR.lerr)}</p>`;if(!A)return picker+range+"<p role='status'>Loading&hellip;</p>";
 const {cur,prev}=gslice(GR.wid,GR.days),n=cur.length,any=gsum(cur,'all')>0;
 const kp=GS.map(s=>{const c=gsum(cur,s[0]),p=gsum(prev,s[0]),d=gdelta(c,p);return `<div class='gk'><span>${s[1]}</span><b>${c}</b><small class='gd${d.d>0?' up':d.d<0?' dn':''}'>${d.t}</small>${gspark(cur.map(r=>r[s[0]]),s[2])}</div>`}),rc=grate(cur),rp=grate(prev),act=cur.filter(r=>gtot(r)>0).length,actp=prev.filter(r=>gtot(r)>0).length;
 kp.push(`<div class='gk'><span>Reaction rate</span><b>${rc==null?'–':rc+'%'}</b><small class='gd${rc!=null&&rp!=null&&rc>rp?' up':rc!=null&&rp!=null&&rc<rp?' dn':''}'>${rc==null||rp==null?'vs before: not enough data':(rc>=rp?'▲ +':'▼ −')+Math.abs(rc-rp)+' points'}</small></div>`,`<div class='gk'><span>Active days</span><b>${act}/${n}</b><small class='gd${act>actp?' up':act<actp?' dn':''}'>${act===actp?'▬ no change':(act>actp?'▲ +':'▼ −')+Math.abs(act-actp)+' days'}</small>${gspark(cur.map(gtot),'#8ec0ff')}</div>`);
 const mets=[['all','All activity'],...GS.map(s=>[s[0],s[1]])],si=GR.sel==null||GR.sel>=n?n-1:GR.sel,sr=cur[si],pr=prev[si],cv=gmet(sr,GR.metric),pv=pr?gmet(pr,GR.metric):null,dd=pv==null?null:gdelta(cv,pv);
 const pk=cur.reduce((b,r)=>gtot(r)>gtot(b)?r:b,cur[0]),sevenC=gsum(cur.slice(-7),'all'),sevenP=gsum(cur.slice(-14,-7),'all'),mom=gdelta(sevenC,sevenP);
 const I=GR.ins[GR.wid+':'+GR.days];
 const trend=`<h3>Trend</h3><div class='vch vadd' role='group' aria-label='Choose what to chart'>${mets.map(m=>`<button class='lk2' data-w='grmet:${m[0]}' aria-pressed='${GR.metric===m[0]}'>${m[1]}</button>`).join('')}</div><button class='lk2' data-w='grsm' aria-pressed='${GR.smooth}'>7-day average: ${GR.smooth?'on':'off'}</button><div class='gbox'>${grTrend(cur,prev,w)}</div><div class='gleg2'><span><i style='background:${GCOL[GR.metric]||'#8ec0ff'}'></i>This period</span><span><i class='gd2'></i>Previous ${GR.days} days</span><span><i class='gdv'></i>Published or updated</span></div><p class='grd' role='status'><b>${fmtD(sr.day)}</b>: ${cv} now${pv==null?'':', '+pv+' on the matching day before ('+dd.t+')'}</p><p class='hint'>Tap the chart to read a day.</p>`;
 return picker+range+(any?'':"<p class='hint'>No activity in this period yet. The numbers stay at zero until people find your idea.</p>")+`<div class='gkg'>${kp.join('')}</div>
 <p class='gsum'><b>Peak day:</b> ${any?fmtD(pk.day)+' ('+gtot(pk)+' actions)':'none yet'}. <b>Momentum:</b> the last 7 days had ${sevenC} actions against ${sevenP} in the 7 days before (${mom.t}).</p>${trend}
 <h3>Journey of a visitor</h3><p class='hint'>How far people go. The small number shows how many step-ups per 100 of the step before. It is a guide, not a promise that one step caused the next.</p>${grFunnel(cur)}
 <h3>Best days of the week</h3><p class='hint'>Total activity for each weekday in this period.</p>${grWeekday(cur)}
 <h3>Calendar of the last 12 weeks</h3>${grHeat(A)}
 <h3>What kind of activity, week by week</h3>${grMix(A)}<div class='vch gleg3'>${GS.map(s=>`<span><i style='background:${s[2]}'></i>${s[1]}</span>`).join('')}</div>
 ${M.length>1?`<h3>Compare your ideas</h3><div class='vtb'><table><thead><tr><th>Idea</th><th>Views</th><th>Reactions</th><th>Trend</th></tr></thead><tbody>${M.slice(0,6).map(x=>{const q=gslice(x.id,GR.days),c=q.cur,v=gsum(c,'views'),re=gsum(c,'resonates')+gsum(c,'comments')+gsum(c,'suggestions'),d=gdelta(gsum(c,'all'),gsum(q.prev,'all'));return `<tr><th><button class='lk2' data-w='grwork:${esc(x.id)}'>${esc((x.title||'Untitled').slice(0,20))}</button></th><td>${GR.all[x.id]?v:'…'}</td><td>${GR.all[x.id]?re:'…'}</td><td>${gspark(c.map(gtot),'#8ec0ff',50,18)} ${esc(d.t)}</td></tr>`}).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`:''}
 <div class='row' style='margin-top:12px'><button class='lk2' data-w='grtbl' aria-expanded='${GR.tbl}'>${GR.tbl?'Hide the numbers':'Show the numbers'}</button><button class='lk2' data-w='grcsv'>Download as CSV</button></div>${GR.tbl?`<div class='vtb'><table><thead><tr><th>Day</th>${GS.map(s=>`<th>${s[1]}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${cur.filter(r=>gtot(r)>0).map(r=>`<tr><th>${fmtD(r.day)}</th>${GS.map(s=>`<td>${r[s[0]]}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')||"<tr><td colspan='6'>No activity.</td></tr>"}</tbody></table></div>`:''}
 <details class='gdt'><summary>What do these words mean?</summary><p><b>Views</b>: people who opened your idea. <b>Searches</b>: times it showed up in someone's search results. <b>Resonates</b>: people who tapped Resonate. <b>Comments</b>: comments and questions. <b>Suggestions</b>: changes people proposed. <b>Reaction rate</b>: resonates, comments and suggestions for every 100 views. <b>Active days</b>: days when anything happened. Days are counted in UTC.</p></details>
 <h3>What is happening, and why?</h3>${GR.err?`<p class='ms er' role='alert'>${esc(GR.err)}</p>`:''}${I?`<div class='gin'><p>${esc(I.summary)}</p>${I.too_early?"<p class='hint'>There is not much activity yet, so this is only a first look.</p>":''}${(I.causes||[]).length?`<h4>Why it may have changed</h4>${I.causes.map(c=>`<div class='zi'><span class='zm'>&bull;</span><div><p>${esc(c.what)}</p><span class='zb'>${esc(c.evidence||'')}</span></div></div>`).join('')}`:''}${(I.next||[]).length?`<h4>What you could try</h4>${I.next.map(x=>`<div class='zi'><span class='zm'>&rarr;</span><div><p>${esc(x)}</p></div></div>`).join('')}`:''}<p class='cn'>AI reading of the numbers, not proof. async function saveNew(b){if(!draft.x.trim()){say('cm','Write your idea first.',1);return}if(!sb||!user){say('cm','You are not signed in.',1);return}
 b.disabled=true;say('cm','Saving...');
 try{
  /* original_expression is sent exactly as typed (not trimmed, not rewritten) and is never included in any later update. */
  const {data,error}=await sb.from('ideas').insert({original_expression:draft.x,working_version:draft.x,title:draft.t.trim()||null,status:'new'}).select(COLS).single();
  if(error||!data){say('cm','Could not save: '+(error?dbErr(error):'no confirmation from the database')+' Your text is still here.',1);return}
  draft={t:'',x:''};storeDraft();lastRow=data;if(ideas)ideas=[data,...ideas];else loadIdeas2();
  delete CI[data.id];setLast(data.id);FX.begin(data.id);FX.warp();zoomNext=true;CIS(data.id).insTried=true;swap('imag',data.id);ciRun('understand',data.id)}
 catch(err){say('cm','Could not save: '+dbErr(err)+' Your text is still here.',1)}
 finally{b.disabled=false}}
async function updIdea(b){const i=findI(cs.id);if(!i||!sb)return false;const v=ed[i.id];if(!v){say('im','No changes to save.');return false}

 b.disabled=false;if(!r.ok){say('im','Could not save: '+r.msg+' Your changes are still here.',1);return false}
